Result: 提供了该领域首个可用的数据集及每个赛道的地面真实标签，使参与者能够评估其解决方案；竞赛最终排名将在结束后公布。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛通过提供标准化数据集和评估框架，推动了AI在儿童步态分析这一新兴领域的发展，为儿童医疗诊断提供了临床相关的批量分析机会。

Abstract: The First AI Children Challenge aims to advance real-world applications of computer vision and AI in child healthcare, child education, and pediatrics. The 2026 CV4CHL edition featured the first track in this domain: Children Gait Visual Analysis. The main goal of Children Gait Visual Analysis is the fine-grained analysis of children's gait behaviors from keypoint sequences. This is still a big challenge for human action recognition. Experienced medical doctors can distinguish these subtle nuances, but none of the people test AI models in this domain. To bridge this gap, we introduce thousands of 2D children keypoint sequences walking around videos across various age groups of children (3-16 years old). There is a significant opportunity for batch analysis of these videos to provide clinically relevant insights into medical diagnosis. The Challenge will be launched with two problem tracks: Edinburgh Visual Gait Score (EVGS) Scoring and Classification of Gait Patterns in Bilateral Spastic Cerebral Palsy. Each track is chosen in consultation with board-certified pediatricians based on the value of potential solutions. With the first available dataset for such tasks and ground truth for each track, the challenge enabled participants to evaluate their solutions. Final rankings will be revealed after the competition concludes, fostering reproducibility and mitigating overfitting.

</details>

*Simiao Sun,Kenneth Ng,Lynn Lee,Astrid Harth,Asami Odate,Aggelos Katsaggelos,Manuel Ballester Matito,Nicholas Eastaugh,Marc Walton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种AI蒸馏框架，通过多模态教师模型（结合视觉嵌入与光照、放大倍率、标本身份等文本嵌入）生成2304维分块教师向量，再用仅接受图像输入的ViT学生模型重建该向量，实现从单一图像中提取丰富且可解释的语义表示，用于颗粒和纤维分散体的显微图像检索与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学显微镜下的颗粒和纤维分散体图像包含复杂的视觉线索，受标本形态、化学组成、放大倍率和光照条件等多因素影响。传统仅依赖图像的方法难以提取足够丰富的语义表示，需要一种能够从纯图像输入中恢复多模态语义信息的方法。

Method: 1) 多模态教师模型将图像视觉嵌入与三种文本嵌入（光照模态、放大倍率、标本身份与形态）结合，利用LongCLIP编码器生成2304维分块结构教师向量；2) 学生ViT+MLP解码器仅从图像输入重建教师向量，使用L1损失确保坐标级保真；3) 通过HDBSCAN聚类得到的伪类进行交叉熵正则化，防止表示坍塌，避免对比负样本挖掘；4) 推理时学生仅需图像输入即可生成紧凑且语义丰富的嵌入。

Result: 该框架在伪类验证准确率上达到约80%，在细粒度标本描述标签的留一法最近邻检索中实现75%的Recall@1，证明了语义锚定使得视觉-only学生模型能获得比纯图像训练更丰富、更具可解释性的表示。

Conclusion: 语义锚定蒸馏框架使纯视觉学生模型能够获取教师的多模态语义内容，生成的嵌入具有可解释性和实用性，可直接应用于异质颗粒与纤维分散体的检索、分类及探索性分析。

Abstract: Optical microscopy of particle and fiber dispersions involves interpreting subtle visual cues influenced by specimen morphology, chemical composition, magnification, and illumination conditions. We introduce an artificial intelligence (AI) distillation framework that extracts semantically rich image embeddings from microscopy images using semantic anchors. A multimodal teacher combines each image's visual embedding with three text embeddings representing illumination modality, magnification, and specimen identity and morphology. Generated by LongCLIP's extended-context text encoder, this yields a 2304-dimensional block-structured teacher vector whose component blocks remain physically interpretable throughout training and inference. A student vision transformer (ViT) with a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) decoder is trained to reconstruct this teacher vector from the image alone, minimizing a mean absolute error (L1) loss that enforces coordinate-level fidelity to the teacher's block structure. A cross-entropy term over pseudo-classes derived from HDBSCAN clustering of the teacher embedding space acts as a collapse-prevention regularizer, enforcing inter-cluster separation without requiring contrastive negative mining. At inference, the student operates on image input alone, producing compact embeddings that recover the full semantic content of the teacher vector. The framework achieves approximately 80% pseudo-class validation accuracy and 75% Recall@1 on fine-grained specimen description labels under leave-one-out nearest-neighbor retrieval. These results demonstrate that semantic anchoring enables a vision-only student to acquire richer and more interpretable representations than image-only training, with direct applicability to retrieval, classification, and exploratory analysis of heterogeneous particle and fiber dispersions.

</details>


### [37] [Decoding Children's Gait Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00371)
*Yifan Shen,Boyi Li,Meihuan Huang,Yuanzhe Liu,Xu Cao,Jinyang Jin,Zhengyuan Li,Anglin Liu,Junho Kim,Jingyuan Zhu,Lan Fangzhou,Jianguo Cao,Jintai Chen,Ismini Lourentzou,James Matthew Rehg*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D传感器步态分析系统昂贵、侵入性强且对年幼受试者不实用；儿童步态异常（如脑瘫、偏瘫）的临床诊断需要更便捷、非侵入性的自动化分析方法。

Method: 构建了一个包含1100+个60FPS视频片段的儿童步态数据集（110名3-17岁受试者，执行5秒"行走"任务，多视角捕捉步态周期）；分析现有步态基础模型和MLLM的不足；提出一个统一的端到端框架用于解码儿科步态的关键组成部分。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有最先进方法（步态基础模型和MLLM）无法有效解决儿童步态的细粒度临床分析问题，而所提出的数据集和框架展示了建立自动化儿童步态评估基线的潜力。

Conclusion: 该数据集有望推动新的研究问题，并为自动化儿童步态评估建立严谨的基准，填补了当前步态分析在临床儿科应用中的空白。

Abstract: We introduce a new problem domain for human action recognition: the fine-grained analysis of children's gait behaviors from standard RGB video. We specifically target the ambulatory patterns of children aged 3-17 years. Such behaviors arise naturally in the diagnosis and treatment of several critical developmental and neuromuscular disorders, such as cerebral palsy and hemiplegia. Despite their clinical value, current 3D sensor-based gait analysis systems are expensive, intrusive, and often impractical for young subjects. To address this, we introduce a new dataset comprising over 1,100 high-frame-rate (60 FPS) video sequences from 110 subjects, accompanied by synchronized, anonymized pose sequences. In each session, the child performs a 5-second "walk-around" task, capturing the gait cycle from multiple viewpoints. Crucially, we demonstrate that current state-of-the-art approaches, including gait foundation models and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), fail to effectively resolve these clinical nuances. We identify the key technical challenges in analyzing these erratic and subtle motor patterns and describe a unified end-to-end framework for decoding fundamental components of pediatric gait. Through comprehensive experimental results, we demonstrate the potential of this dataset to drive novel research questions and establish a rigorous baseline for automated child gait assessment.

</details>


### [38] [Boosting Generalizable Depth Estimation in Endoscopy by Mixture of Lightweight Experts and Intrinsic Image Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00415)
*Liangjing Shao,Beilei Cui,Yiming Huang,Changjing Liu,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EndoMINI的自监督框架，用于内窥镜手术场景中的深度估计。该框架包含混合低秩专家（MiLoRE）模块实现参数高效微调和场景自适应，以及内在图像对齐（IIA）机制用于缓解内窥镜中光照反射的干扰。在SCARED、Hamlyn和SERV-CT数据集上的实验证明了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜手术中的深度估计对于3D感知至关重要，但光照干扰和不同内窥镜场景的特征多样性仍然是泛化深度估计和自运动估计的主要挑战。

Method: 提出EndoMINI自监督框架，包含两个核心贡献：(1) 混合低秩专家（MiLoRE）用于参数高效微调，增强模型对不同特征场景的适应能力；(2) 内在图像对齐（IIA）引入训练损失，通过新型内在图像分解网络缓解内窥镜中光反射的影响。

Result: 在SCARED数据集上进行了监督深度估计评估，在Hamlyn和SERV-CT数据集上进行了零样本深度估计评估，与最先进方法相比，实验结果展现了突出的性能表现。

Conclusion: 实验验证了所提模型在深度估计任务上的优越性，并证明了MiLoRE和IIA两个主要贡献的有效性。

Abstract: Depth estimation is a significant task for 3D perception in endoscopic surgeries. However, illumination interference and feature diversity in various endoscopic scenes are still challenges for generalizable depth estimation and ego-motion estimation. Based on this, a novel self-supervised framework, EndoMINI, is proposed for depth estimation in endoscopic scenes. Specifically, mixture of low-rank experts (MiLoRE) is proposed to perform parameter-efficient fine-tuning, which can also boost the model adaptation to scenes with different characteristics. Meanwhile, an intrinsic image alignment (IIA) is introduced into the training loss to alleviate the influence of light reflectance in endoscopy with a novel intrinsic image decomposition network. The proposed method is evaluated on SCARED datasets for supervised depth estimation, and two endoscopic datasets, Hamlyn and SERV-CT, for zero-shot depth estimation, compared with state-of-the-art works as well. The experimental results demonstrate outstanding performance of the proposed model and the effects of the main contributions.

</details>


### [39] [Poplar: A Scalable Pipeline for Human-Centric Image Dataset Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00440)
*Zhishan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Poplar，一个可复现的"Specify-Render-Inspect"管线，用于合成高质量人像数据集。通过结构化属性采样、真实性适配的图像生成和统一的视觉-语言审查，构建了包含9,401个精选人像图文对的Poplar-9K数据集（接受率79.9%），并开源了管线、配置、提示词和审查记录等全部资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像生成器可以生成逼真的人像图片，但合成一个有用的数据集不同于合成单张成功图像。人像数据集需要覆盖多样化的人和场景、避免不合理的属性组合、保持日常摄影特征，并在大规模下暴露质量控制决策问题。

Method: Poplar采用三段式管线：(1) Specify阶段在常识约束下采样结构化属性并将其转化为摄影导向的提示词；(2) Render阶段使用经过真实性适配的图像生成器，在构图感知的宽高比下生成并重试明显技术失败；(3) Inspect阶段对每个候选图像应用统一的视觉-语言审查，保留原始提示词的同时拒绝内在图像缺陷或提示词不匹配。

Result: 利用Poplar构建了Poplar-9K数据集，包含9,401个精选的人像图像-文本对，从11,765个被审查的候选中保留（79.9%接受率）。数据集连同管线、配置、不可变生成提示词和可审计的检查记录一起发布。

Conclusion: Poplar提供了一个紧凑、可复现且可审计的资源，用于构建可定制的人像数据集，解决了大规模人像数据合成中的质量控制挑战。

Abstract: Recent image generators can synthesize convincing human-centric images, yet producing a useful collection remains different from producing a single successful image. A human-centric dataset must cover varied people and contexts, avoid implausible attribute combinations, preserve an everyday photographic character, and expose quality-control decisions at scale. We present Poplar, a reproducible Specify--Render--Inspect pipeline for human-centric image dataset synthesis. Specify samples structured attributes under commonsense constraints and verbalizes them as photography-oriented prompts. Render uses a realism-adapted image generator across composition-aware aspect ratios and retries obvious technical failures. Inspect applies a single structured vision--language review to each candidate, preserving the original prompt while rejecting intrinsic image defects or material prompt mismatches. Using Poplar, we construct Poplar-9K: 9,401 curated human-centric image--text pairs retained from 11,765 reviewed candidates (79.9\% acceptance). We release the dataset together with the pipeline, configurations, immutable generation prompts, and auditable inspection records as a compact resource for building customizable human-centric collections.

</details>


### [40] [Beyond Static Anchors: Bounded Prototype Conditioning for Language-Free Medical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00442)
*Yibo Wan,Jinyu Cai,Seekiong-Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReCAP是一个无语言框架的医学异常检测方法，通过输入条件化的视觉原型替代静态参考锚点，在六个医学基准上实现了最佳零样本和少样本性能，同时将推理延迟降低了70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP-based方法在医学异常检测中依赖固定的文本提示或学习到的视觉token作为正常/异常参考，这些静态参考在跨域医学影像场景中难以可靠迁移到未见过的目标域。

Method: ReCAP提出两个关键组件：(1) 通过有界门控调制为每张输入图像重新居中分离的正常和异常视觉原型，实现查询自适应的异常评分，同时约束上下文引起的原型漂移；(2) 针对少样本场景，引入非参数化的正常参考记忆库，保留实例级目标域变异以补充条件原型分支。

Result: 在六个医学基准上，ReCAP在所有零样本设置的图像级AUROC和24个少样本设置中的23个达到最佳，并在三个分割数据集上的零样本像素级AUROC全部达到最优。相比最快基线，推理延迟降低超过70%。

Conclusion: ReCAP通过去除对文本提示和测试时梯度更新的依赖，以输入条件化的视觉原型实现了高效、可泛化的跨域医学异常检测，在性能和效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Medical anomaly detection identifies abnormal images and localizes lesions under scarce supervision while generalizing across organs and modalities. Existing CLIP-based methods reduce annotation requirements through vision--language alignment, but their normal and abnormal references, whether text prompts or learned visual tokens, remain fixed across test images. Such static references may not transfer reliably to unseen targets in a cross-domain medical imaging scenario. To address this, we propose ReCAP, a language-free framework that replaces static anchors with input-conditioned visual prototypes. ReCAP re-centers separated normal and abnormal prototypes for each image through a bounded gated modulation, enabling query-adaptive anomaly scoring while constraining context-induced prototype drift. For the few-shot setting, we introduce a non-parametric normal-reference memory to preserve instance-level target-domain variation and complement the conditional prototype branch. Across six medical benchmarks, ReCAP achieves the best image-level AUROC on all zero-shot and 23 of 24 few-shot settings, and the best zero-shot pixel-level AUROC on all three segmentation datasets. Particularly, it reduces inference latency by over 70% compared to the fastest baseline, without text prompts or test-time gradient updates.

</details>


### [41] [Reconstruction-Shift Discrimination via Mask-Guided Latent Diffusion for Medical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00444)
*Yibo Wan,Jinyu Cai,Yunhe Zhang,Yi Bin,See-kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出判别式掩码引导扩散（DMD）框架，结合残差定位与重建偏移判别用于无监督医学异常检测，在五个数据集上取得最优综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建和扩散的医学异常检测方法仅利用原始图像与重建图像之间的残差作为异常证据，但残差存在歧义：表达力强的模型可能保留病理性结构，而良性解剖变异、成像噪声和采集差异也会产生大的重建误差。这些问题导致仅依赖残差的检测方法不够可靠。

Method: DMD首先学习正常图像的紧凑量化潜在表示，然后通过局部掩码扰动选取的潜在区域，利用潜在扩散模型重建受扰动的表示。重建结果与原始正常图像配对，构成一个自监督分类任务。推理时，分类器提供图像级异常分数，输入与扩散重建之间的残差提供像素级异常图。

Result: 在涵盖脑MRI、乳房超声和胸部X光片的五个数据集上的实验表明，DMD在总体性能上优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: DMD通过将残差定位与重建偏移判别相结合，有效克服了单一残差信号的歧义问题，为无监督医学异常检测提供了一种更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Unsupervised medical anomaly detection learns normal anatomical patterns from healthy training images and identifies deviations at test time. Reconstruction-based and diffusion-based methods commonly use the difference between an input image and its reconstruction as anomaly evidence. However, this residual can be ambiguous. Expressive models may preserve pathological structures, while benign anatomical variation, imaging noise, and acquisition differences may also produce large reconstruction errors. We propose discriminative mask-guided diffusion (DMD), a medical anomaly detection framework that complements residual-based localization with reconstruction-shift discrimination. DMD first learns a compact quantized latent representation of normal images. Localized masks then perturb selected latent regions, and a latent diffusion model reconstructs the perturbed representations. The resulting reconstructions are paired with their original normal images to define a self-supervised classification task. At inference, the classifier provides a learned image-level anomaly score, while the residual between the input and its diffusion-based reconstruction yields a pixel-level anomaly map. Experiments on five datasets spanning brain MRI, breast ultrasound, and chest radiography show that DMD achieves the best overall performance among the state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [42] [Structured Proxy Features for Multimodal NSCLC Survival Prediction from Pretreatment CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00446)
*Huu Phong Nguyen,Delower Hossain,Ehsan Saghapour,Zhandos Sembay,Jake Y. Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出通过仿真生成的代理特征增强多模态NSCLC生存预测，在Lung1队列上取得C-index 0.641（最佳0.662），优于此前文献结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌致死率高，NSCLC生存分层因肿瘤异质性难以准确刻画。现有影像组学和深度学习方法将影像特征视为独立量，忽略了肿瘤特征间的结构化交互，需要更有效的表征手段。

Method: 结合CT影像（Transformer Masked Autoencoder）、影像组学、临床变量，以及由细胞自动机仿真生成的6个代理特征（捕获异质性-形态交互），构建四模态融合模型进行生存预测。

Result: 在Lung1队列（n=390）上，四模态融合C-index为0.641（iAUC 0.731, log-rank p<0.001），优于先前多模态结果（C-index 0.631）；探索性系数优化后最佳C-index达0.662（iAUC 0.748）。

Conclusion: 仿真派生代理特征在Lung1基准上提供了互补的预测信息，有助于增强NSCLC生存预测能力。

Abstract: Lung cancer results in roughly 1.8 million fatalities annually worldwide, with non-small cell lung cancer (NSCLC) comprising the majority of cases. Despite advancements in treatment, survival stratification remains challenging due to intratumoral heterogeneity inadequately captured by conventional descriptors. Standard radiomic and deep learning techniques regard imaging features as independent quantities, overlooking structured interactions between tumor characteristics. We evaluate whether structured proxy features can enhance multimodal NSCLC survival prediction by augmenting pretreatment computed tomography (CT) representations, radiomics, and clinical variables with six simulation-derived features designed to capture interactions between heterogeneity and morphology. A radiomic-parameterized cellular automaton generates growth-rate and necrosis-ratio proxy features from baseline CT by using entropy and sphericity to compute low-dimensional proxy parameters. The imaging backbone is a Transformer-based Masked Autoencoder (TMAE), which was chosen after a systematic evaluation with alternative encoders within the same pipeline and provides attention-based visualizations that highlight tumor regions receiving higher model attention. On the public Lung1 cohort (n = 390), the primary four-modality fusion attained a C-index of 0.641 (iAUC 0.731, log-rank p < 0.001). The primary result compares favorably with prior multimodal results on Lung1 (C-index 0.631; iAUC 0.592 [15]) under a comparable evaluation protocol, while a separate exploratory coefficient-optimization analysis achieved a best observed C-index of 0.662 (iAUC 0.748). These results indicate that, in addition to conventional radiomic, deep, and clinical representations within the Lung1 benchmark, simulation-derived proxy features may provide complementary predictive information within this fixed Lung1 benchmark.

</details>


### [43] [Scene2Sound: Auditory-Grounded Soundscape Generation for 3D Gaussian Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00463)
*Masaki Yoshida,Ren Togo,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Scene2Sound框架，首次实现3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）世界中与空间一致的声音景观生成。通过训练无关的听觉锚定方法，利用视觉语言模型识别发声物体，并通过高斯集匹配将多视角检测关联为3D实例，使生成的音频能够随听众移动保持空间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS能够创建逼真的3D世界模拟，但这些世界是"无声的"。现有音频生成方法只基于单张图像或单一视角条件生成，导致声音绑定于特定观察视角，当听众在3D世界中移动时声音无法保持一致。作者希望填补这一空白，实现3D世界中空间一致的声音景观生成。

Method: Scene2Sound采用训练无关框架，流程包括：（1）选择联合覆盖场景的多视角；（2）利用视觉语言模型识别各视角中的发声物体；（3）通过高斯集匹配（测量每个检测对应渲染的高斯集重叠度）将多视角检测关联为3D实例；（4）为每个声源生成音频，由标准基于对象的音频引擎在任意听众姿态下实时空间化。还提出两个空间一致性评估指标。

Result: 在生成的3DGS世界及由真实世界360度捕获生成的3DGS场景上，Scene2Sound在保持与强基线相当的音频质量的同时，实现了单视角和单全景图方法无法达到的空间一致性；用户研究确认了其感知上的优势。

Conclusion: Scene2Sound通过听觉锚定和高斯集匹配，首次实现了3DGS世界中空间一致的声景观生成，在无需额外训练的情况下超越了现有单视角方法的空间一致性局限，为空间音频与3D渲染的结合提供了新的范式。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) turns captured or generated imagery into photorealistic 3D world simulations that users can freely explore, yet these worlds remain silent. Because existing audio generation methods condition on a single image or viewpoint, their sound is tied to that observation and cannot stay consistent while a listener moves. We introduce the task of generating a spatially consistent soundscape for a given 3DGS world through auditory grounding, identifying which objects in the world should emit sound and anchoring each to a persistent 3D position, and present Scene2Sound, a training-free framework built on this grounding. From the input world alone, our pipeline selects viewpoints that jointly cover the scene, identifies sound-emitting objects with a vision-language model, and associates the multi-view detections into 3D instances through Gaussian set matching, which measures the overlap between the Gaussian sets that render each detection. Each source then receives generated audio that a standard object-based audio engine spatializes in real time at arbitrary listener poses. We further propose two spatial-consistency metrics, one testing whether rendered audio responds consistently to listener motion and one testing whether the claimed sources are supported by views held out from their placement. On a curated set of generated 3DGS worlds and on 3DGS scenes generated from real-world 360-degree captures, Scene2Sound preserves the audio quality of strong per-viewpoint baselines while remaining spatially consistent where per-viewpoint and single-panorama pipelines do not, and a user study confirms the perceptual benefit. Project page: https://masaki-lmd.github.io/scene2sound/.

</details>


### [44] [CrossProjection: Geometric Grounding Beyond Viewpoint Change in Architectural Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00473)
*Kaho Li,Pengyu Zeng,Yuqin Dai,Jun Yin,Tianjing Feng,Shuai Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CrossProjection，一个用于评估视觉语言模型在异构建筑设计图纸（平/剖面为切割视图、立面为投影视图）中保持组件身份和外部化几何能力的新型诊断基准。在23套真实图纸、1954个分类条件下，GPT-5.5达到82.4%准确率但自由几何定位仍不可靠（PCK@.05低至54-76%），而Qwen和GLM表现更差。人类专家可达87.3-93.3%准确率，证明任务可行。核心结论是分类正确性不代表候选无关的空间定位可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑设计图纸的多视图推理违反了常规多视角推理假设（相机运动无法解释平/剖面与立面之间的外观变化），现有视觉语言模型在此类异构视图下能否保持组件身份和几何外化能力尚不明确，需要专门的诊断工具。

Method: 提出CrossProjection基准，通过三个任务维度（Matching匹配、Registration配准、Geometric Grounding几何接地）评估模型，涵盖分类判断、候选选择和自由点/线/区域定位。使用固定分母评分、锚点标记和哈希锁定的可复用测试集，并在23套真实图纸上对多个模型及人类专家进行对照测试。

Result: GPT-5.5在分类任务上达82.4%，Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct为62.2%，GLM-4.5V为57.2%。但自由定位表现脆弱：GPT自然图纸点/区域PCK@.05为54-76%，Qwen仅8-10%，GLM为14-36%；线端点PCK分别为22%、4%和0%。坐标网格可恢复部分GPT的点/区域精度但无法改善线条。三位建筑专家达87.3-93.3%分类准确率和76-92%的GT区域命中率。

Conclusion: 分类正确性或候选选择成功不能证明模型具备候选无关的、可靠的显式几何接地能力。对于图纸引导的CAD/BIM系统，不应将分类正确性视为空间可靠性的证据。本文通过审计痕迹（可复用锚点、固定分母评分、哈希锁定工件）为这一能力差距提供了可追溯的评估框架。

Abstract: Architectural drawings violate the usual assumption behind multi-view reasoning: plans and sections are cuts, while elevations are facade projections, so corresponding components change appearance in ways camera motion cannot explain. We introduce CrossProjection, an anchor-grounded diagnostic of whether vision-language models preserve component identity and externalize geometry across heterogeneous architectural views. It evaluates Matching, Registration, and Geometric Grounding through categorical judgments, candidate selection, and free point, line, and region localization.
  Across 23 real drawing sets and 1,954 categorical conditions per model, GPT-5.5 scores 82.4%, Qwen3-VL-32B-Instruct 62.2%, and GLM-4.5V 57.2%. A matched 200-target study crosses natural and vector-text-suppressed drawings with closed-candidate and free-geometry outputs. Candidate-supported performance is often higher, but free localization remains fragile: on natural drawings, point/region PCK@.05 is 54-76% for GPT, 8-10% for Qwen, and 14-36% for GLM; line endpoint PCK@.05 is 22%, 4%, and 0%. A coordinate grid recovers some GPT point/region precision but not lines. Three architecture-trained participants reach 87.3-93.3% categorical accuracy and 76-92% GT-region hit, supporting task feasibility rather than a population-level human ceiling.
  Because the categorical families do not form a same-item Matching-Registration contrast and interface controls alter multiple burdens, we avoid mechanistic claims. The supported conclusion is narrower: closed-choice or marked-element success does not entail reliable explicit geometric grounding. For drawing-guided CAD/BIM systems, categorical correctness should not be treated as evidence of candidate-free spatial reliability. Reusable on-sheet anchors, fixed-denominator scoring, and hash-locked artifacts establish an audit trail for this gap.

</details>


### [45] [DreamTraj: Generating 6-DoF Object Trajectories by Reading Unrendered Video Diffusion Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00486)
*Tongsheng Ding,Zhen Luo,Yixuan Yang,Boyu Wang,Luyang Xie,Jinyu Yang,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MOVE数据集（5,038条带细粒度自然语言指令的对象中心轨迹）和DreamTraj方法，后者通过从冻结的图像到视频扩散模型的中间表示中直接解码6-DoF对象轨迹，无需视频、深度或CAD模型，比生成-提取管线快4.6倍，在平移和旋转预测上均达到最新最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集缺乏细粒度的语言到运动标注，且已有轨迹预测器要么依赖特权输入（视频、深度、CAD模型），要么通过生成视频后经昂贵的感知管线恢复运动，制约了感知-行动闭环的进展。

Method: DreamTraj方法从单张RGB图像和任务指令出发，利用冻结的图像到视频扩散模型，在早期去噪步骤读取其内部表征中的运动信息，并通过轻量的流匹配Reader解码查询-键注意力轨迹和池化隐藏状态为相对6-DoF位姿。同时构建MOVE数据集以弥补监督信号缺口。

Result: DreamTraj在平移和旋转预测上均超越使用多帧或特权输入的预测器，是首个直接从视频扩散中间表示解码6-DoF轨迹而非从生成的像素中恢复的方法，并且运行速度快4.6倍。

Conclusion: 通过MOVE数据集填补了语言到运动标注的空白，并验证了直接从扩散模型内部表征解码运动轨迹的有效性，为无需特权输入的6-DoF轨迹预测提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of object trajectories during manipulation is essential for closing the perception-action loop. Progress is limited on two fronts: available datasets lack fine-grained language-to-motion annotations, and existing predictors either rely on privileged inputs such as video, depth, or CAD models, or recover motion from fully generated videos through costly, error-prone perception pipelines. We close the supervision gap with the MOVE dataset, 5,038 object-centric egocentric trajectories, each paired with a fine-grained natural-language instruction rather than a coarse verb-noun label. We further propose DreamTraj, which predicts a 6-DoF object trajectory from a single RGB image and a task instruction, requiring no video, depth, or CAD model at inference: rather than generating a video, it reads motion from the internal representations of a frozen image-to-video diffusion model at an early denoising step. A lightweight flow-matching Reader decodes query-key attention tracks and pooled hidden states into relative 6-DoF poses. To our knowledge, this is the first approach to directly decode object 6-DoF trajectories from intermediate video diffusion representations rather than generated pixels. DreamTraj sets a new state of the art on both translation and rotation against forecasters that consume multi-frame or privileged inputs, and runs 4.6x faster than generate-then-extract pipelines.

</details>


### [46] [Practical Noise Modeling for SPAD Intensity Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00489)
*Wendi Liu,Yujie Lu,Zengxi Zhang,Haiyang Jiang,Weihang Ran,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套面向SPAD强度成像的实用噪声建模与校准框架，包括二项观测过程的二元帧累积前向模型、专用的校准流程、计数域噪声合成管线以及SPAD特有的暗阴影校正（SPAD-DSC），并在真实SPAD数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPAD相机在低光和高动态范围强度成像方面前景广阔，但传感器特有的复杂噪声严重限制了实际应用。与TCSPC系统不同，SPAD相机在强度成像模式下仅记录每个门控周期内是否发生至少一次探测，缺乏光子时间戳，导致显式噪声分解十分困难。因此需要一种实用的噪声建模与校准框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出前向模型：以二项观测过程描述二元帧累积，将信号无关的暗噪声建模为曝光相关的纯暗计数项加上曝光无关的暗帧偏置项，并纳入像素级响应非均匀性。2) 设计专用校准流程用于模型参数估计。3) 基于校准构建计数域噪声合成管线用于网络训练。4) 设计SPAD特定的暗阴影校正（SPAD-DSC）以在训练前去除大部分系统噪声。5) 构建真实世界SPAD强度数据集进行测试。

Result: 在构建的真实SPAD强度数据集上进行了实验，实验结果证明了所提噪声模型的优越性，在SPAD强度去噪任务上取得了优于现有方法的表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的噪声建模与校准框架能够有效解决SPAD强度成像中传感器特定噪声难以建模的问题，通过结合物理意义明确的噪声模型与数据驱动的去噪网络，为SPAD强度去噪提供了实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) cameras are promising for low-light and high-dynamic-range intensity imaging, but their practical use is limited by complex sensor-specific noise. Unlike time-correlated single-photon counting (TCSPC) systems, SPAD cameras record whether at least one detection occurred in each gate without photon timestamps in intensity imaging mode, making explicit noise decomposition difficult. We present a practical noise modeling and calibration framework for SPAD intensity denoising. Our forward model describes binary-frame accumulation with a Binomial observation process, models signal-independent dark noise as an exposure-dependent pure dark count term plus an exposure-independent dark-frame bias term, and incorporates pixel-wise response non-uniformity. We design a dedicated calibration procedure for the proposed model and use it to build a count-domain noise-synthesis pipeline for network training. For denoising, we further design a SPAD-specific dark-shading correction (SPAD-DSC) to remove most systematic noise before network training. We construct a real-world SPAD intensity dataset for testing. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the proposed noise model.

</details>


### [47] [Image-Space Rule Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00490)
*Misora Sugiyama,Toya Oyama,Hirokatsu Kataoka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WISRD基准，用于评估图像编辑模型在图像空间中端到端解决视觉规则发现问题的能力，涵盖11个核心任务和4个压力测试。实验发现Nano Banana Pro表现最佳（48.7%），但整体性能仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究图像编辑模型能否像人类完成工作表测试（如IQ测试）那样，直接从图像中读取指令、识别问题、推断答案并完成端到端求解，而不是仅依赖外部文本提示。

Method: 构建WISRD（Worksheet Image-Space Rule Discovery）基准，包含11个核心任务（覆盖局部标记、填充、复制、计数、无编辑抑制等）在8种信息条件下，另加4个辅助推理压力测试（多步空间操作、抽象模式推理、逻辑推断、约束求解）。

Result: Nano Banana Pro取得最高分，Auto-Strict代理通过率为48.7%；Qwen-Image-Edit为13.4%，FLUX.2 Klein 4B（API和开源）约11%，InstructPix2Pix为0%。在补充诊断中，Nano Banana Pro在4x4数独上达70.0%，在RAVEN模式发现任务上为22.9%。

Conclusion: 当前图像编辑模型已能在一定程度上利用图像内渲染指令（即使无外部提示或提示很笼统），但总体端到端视觉推理能力仍相当有限，距离人类级工作表测试表现差距显著。

Abstract: Can image-editing models discover visual rules in image space and complete problem-solving end-to-end? We tackle this question in the spirit of a human worksheet test (e.g., an IQ test), using problems that require models to read image-based instructions, recognize the problem, infer the answer, bind it to the correct destination, control output count, suppress unnecessary edits, and preserve the input and format. We introduce WISRD, a Worksheet Image-Space Rule Discovery benchmark with 11 core tasks under eight information conditions, spanning localized marking, filling, copying, counting, and no-edit suppression, together with four supplementary reasoning-stress probes for multi-step spatial manipulation, abstract pattern reasoning, logical inference, and constraint-based problem solving. We identify three key findings as follows. (i) Among the frontier image-editing models evaluated, Nano Banana Pro achieves the highest score. On the shared V0--V3 no-reference subset, the Auto-Strict proxy pass rates are 48.7% for Nano Banana Pro, 13.4\% for Qwen-Image-Edit, 11.5% for FLUX.2 Klein 4B API, 11.3% for FLUX.2 Klein 4B open-weight, and 0.0% for InstructPix2Pix. (ii) Analysis reveals that current image-editing models can partially rely on rendered in-image instructions even when the external prompt is absent or merely generic. (iii) In small supplementary diagnostics, Nano Banana Pro achieves 70.0% on 4-by-4 Sudoku and 22.9% on public RAVEN pattern-discovery items in image space.

</details>


### [48] [Optical Flow from Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00499)
*Wendi Liu,Weichao Zeng,Weihang Ran,Yujie Lu,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QuantaFlow是首个直接从SPAD光子流估计稠密光流的方法，将SPAD表示构建嵌入迭代光流精化中，通过运动感知聚合和自适应多尺度融合解决稀疏光子与运动模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机在高速和低光照场景下因帧率与灵敏度限制导致运动模糊和欠曝光；SPAD相机虽有单光子灵敏度和极细时间采样，但其高帧率二值光子流中单个切片过于稀疏，难以进行稠密对应匹配。现有时间聚合方法在固定坐标累积光会模糊运动结构，虽然运动感知聚合可减少模糊，但其依赖待估计的光流本身，形成循环依赖。

Method: QuantaFlow将SPAD表示构建嵌入到迭代光流精化框架中。每轮迭代中，当前光流对源和目标的子流内切片进行粗对齐；通过光子通量变换构建包含强度和结构线索的多尺度表示；自适应多尺度融合在每像素平衡光子噪声与残余运动模糊；融合表示驱动特征扭曲光流更新，精化后的光流再指导下一轮表示构建。此外还构建了用于SPAD光流训练与评估的合成数据集。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界SPAD数据上的实验证明QuantaFlow的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将表示构建与光流估计迭代耦合，QuantaFlow打破了运动感知聚合对流估计的依赖循环，实现了直接从SPAD流进行稠密光流估计，为高速低光照场景的光流计算提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Optical flow remains challenging in high-speed and low-light scenes, where the limited frame rate and sensitivity of conventional cameras lead to motion blur and underexposure. Single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) cameras offer single-photon sensitivity and extremely fine temporal sampling. However, individual slices in these high FPS binary photon streams are too sparse for dense correspondence. Temporal aggregation can provide the spatial cues required by optical flow, but accumulating photons at fixed coordinates blurs moving structures. Motion-aware aggregation can reduce this blur, yet it depends on the flow being estimated. To address this dependency, we propose QuantaFlow, the first method for dense optical flow directly from SPAD streams. Instead of constructing a fixed input representation, QuantaFlow embeds SPAD representation construction into iterative flow refinement. At each iteration, the current flow coarsely aligns the slices within the source and target sub-streams. A photon-flux transformation then constructs multi-scale representations containing intensity and structural cues, while adaptive multi-scale fusion balances photon noise and residual motion blur at each pixel. The fused representations drive a feature-warping flow update, and the refined flow guides representation construction in the next iteration. We further construct a synthetic dataset for SPAD optical-flow training and evaluation. Experiments on the synthetic dataset and real-world SPAD data demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of QuantaFlow.

</details>


### [49] [SpatialAfford: Teaching Compact VLMs Where to Look and Where to Ground for Affordance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00502)
*Yufei Zhang,Chenlu Zhan,Donghui Sun,Xiaoxin Chen,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialAfford是一个两阶段框架，通过空间注意力对齐（SAA）和空间感知GRPO，先教会紧凑视觉语言模型"看哪里"再"指哪里"，从而提升具身场景中的可操作区域定位能力，使4B模型超越7B+基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可操作区域定位比通用视觉定位更困难，因为目标区域更小、更模糊、更依赖任务上下文。现有的序列级监督和强化学习虽然改善了坐标预测质量，但紧凑自回归VLM在生成坐标前缺乏可靠的可操作感知视觉焦点——模型能生成更好的坐标token，但跨模态注意力仍然弥散且未能弱锚定到真实的可操作证据上。

Method: 提出SpatialAfford两阶段框架：第一阶段通过空间注意力对齐（SAA）将注意力与真实可操作区域对齐；第二阶段使用空间感知GRPO（Spatial-Aware GRPO）细化坐标预测。整体思路是先显式教会模型在哪里看，再优化在哪里定位，将可操作定位从纯输出约束的目标转变为注意力锚定的空间推理。

Result: 在ShareRobot-Bench、ReasonAff和PartAfford三个基准上，SpatialAfford一致性地提升了可操作定位性能，紧凑的4B模型超越了更强的7B+基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过将注意力对齐前置到坐标优化之前，SpatialAfford有效解决了紧凑VLM在可操作定位中注意力弥散的问题，证明了"先对齐注意力、再优化空间定位"这一两阶段策略的优势，并且紧凑模型也能在性能上超越更大规模的模型。

Abstract: Affordance grounding aims to localize the functional region for interaction, such as the handle to grasp or the button to press, rather than the whole object. This makes it more challenging than generic visual grounding because the target region is smaller, more ambiguous, and more dependent on task context, especially for compact vision-language models (VLMs) used in embodied settings. Recent sequence-level supervision and reinforcement learning improve coordinate prediction quality, yet compact autoregressive VLMs still lack reliable affordance-aware visual focus before coordinate generation: the model can produce better coordinate tokens while its cross-modal attention remains diffuse and weakly anchored to the true affordance evidence. To address it, we propose SpatialAfford, a two-stage framework that first aligns attention to the ground-truth affordance region through Spatial Attention Alignment (SAA), then refines coordinate prediction with Spatial-Aware GRPO. By explicitly teaching the model where to look before optimizing where to ground, SpatialAfford turns affordance grounding from a purely output-constrained objective into attention-grounded spatial reasoning. Across ShareRobot-Bench, ReasonAff, and PartAfford, SpatialAfford consistently improves affordance grounding, with a compact 4B model outperforming stronger 7B+ baselines.

</details>


### [50] [RadYOLO: Computationally Efficient 3D Object Detection and Segmentation in CT and MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00508)
*Kai Geissler,Laurens Müller-Groh,Hans Meine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RadYOLO，一个面向三维医学图像的YOLO11扩展模型，在保持高检测性能的同时显著降低计算成本，推理速度比nnU-Net快8-46倍，比nnDetection更快，甚至CPU上运行也比nnU-Net在GPU上更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学图像检测模型（如nnU-Net、nnDetection）计算成本高，缺乏同时具备高检测性能、广泛适用性和快速推理能力的模型，难以在资源受限的硬件（如临床和边缘设备）上部署。

Method: 将YOLO11扩展到3D医学图像领域，提出RadYOLO模型，并在包含CT和MRI的五个数据集上与nnU-Net和nnDetection进行系统比较，覆盖不同目标尺寸和出现频率。

Result: RadYOLO在五个数据集中的四个上检测性能超过nnDetection，一个相当；在病灶检测任务上优于nnU-Net；在粗略目标定位场景下与nnU-Net相当或更好。GPU推理速度比nnU-Net快8-46倍，比nnDetection速度提升更大，CPU上运行也快于nnU-Net在GPU上的表现。

Conclusion: RadYOLO在检测性能与推理速度之间取得了优异平衡，特别适合临床部署和边缘设备使用场景，为3D医学图像检测提供了一个高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Object detection and segmentation in three-dimensional medical images is a very active area of research. However, most proposed deep learning models carry a high computational cost, and only few aim to be broadly applicable, achieve high detection performance, and remain fast to execute on resource-constrained hardware. To address this gap, we present RadYOLO, a 3D extension of YOLO11 tailored to medical images. We compare it with nnU-Net and nnDetection on five datasets comprising CT and MRI data with varying object sizes and prevalence. RadYOLO's detection performance surpasses that of nnDetection on four of five datasets and is comparable on one. Compared to nnU-Net, RadYOLO performs better on lesion detection tasks, while nnU-Net excels at detecting large organs when precise localization is required. When rough object localization is sufficient, RadYOLO matches or outperforms nnU-Net on all five datasets. Regarding inference time, RadYOLO is 8-46x faster than nnU-Net on a GPU. Compared to nnDetection the speedup is even higher. When executed on a CPU, RadYOLO's inference runs within seconds (still faster than nnU-Net on a GPU) offering a significant advantage for clinical and edge-device deployment.
  RadYOLO repository: https://github.com/FraunhoferMEVIS/RadYOLO

</details>


### [51] [Test-time Adaptation of Pelvic Bone Segmentation Models via Dynamic Reliability-Guided](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00510)
*Ling Ren,Chao Deng,Ziming Wang,Yuecong Xu,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReGA的新型闭环动态可靠性引导测试时自适应（TTA）框架，用于骨盆CT图像分割，以解决跨中心域偏移导致的分割性能下降问题。ReGA通过引入分割推断一致性评估（SICE）作为伪标签可靠性准则，结合信任加权细化模块、置信度加权区域级对比学习以及教师-学生（TS）方案，在三个异构三维骨盆CT数据集上优于现有TTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的骨盆骨分割对机器人辅助骨盆创伤手术至关重要，但跨医院的域偏移导致源域模型在新医院性能严重下降。现有TTA方法在处理骨盆骨分割时面临三个关键难题：边界退化、域偏移下的解剖不一致性以及体素级类别不平衡。

Method: ReGA框架包含以下核心组件：(1) 提出分割推断一致性评估（SICE）——基于dropout集成预测，联合度量区域重叠和边界偏差的伪标签可靠性准则；(2) 基于SICE的信任加权细化模块，自适应更新特征以缓解伪标签中的边界错误；(3) 置信度加权区域级对比学习策略，强制解剖一致性；(4) 采用教师-学生（TS）方案缓解体素级类别不平衡问题。

Result: 在三个异构三维骨盆CT数据集上的实验表明，ReGA一致性地优于现有最先进的TTA方法，能够有效实现源训练骨盆骨分割模型对未见临床域的适应。

Conclusion: ReGA通过闭环动态可靠性引导机制有效解决了TTA在骨盆骨分割中的边界退化、解剖不一致和类别不平衡问题，显著提升了跨中心域适应的鲁棒性，为机器人辅助骨盆创伤手术提供了可靠的自动化分割支持。

Abstract: Reliable pelvic bone segmentation (PBS) from CT is essential for robot-assisted pelvic trauma surgery, yet deploying a source-trained model to a new hospital suffers from severe performance degradation due to cross-center domain shifts. While test-time adaptation (TTA) enables online model adaptation without accessing source data, existing methods show limited effectiveness for PBS, facing challenges including boundary degradation, anatomical inconsistency under domain shifts, and voxel-level class imbalance. To address these challenges, we propose a novel closed-loop dynamic Reliability-Guided TTA framework (ReGA) for PBS. Specifically, we introduce a pseudo-label reliability criterion termed Segmentation Inference Consistency Evaluation (SICE), which jointly measures region overlap and boundary deviation via dropout-based ensemble predictions. Based on SICE, a trust-weighted refinement module adaptively updates features to mitigate boundary errors in pseudo-labels. Furthermore, a confidence-weighted region-level contrastive learning strategy is proposed to enforce anatomical consistency. Finally, ReGA follows the teacher-student (TS) scheme to alleviate voxel-level class imbalance. Experiments on three heterogeneous 3D pelvic CT datasets demonstrate that ReGA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art TTA methods, enabling effective adaptation of the source-trained PBS model to unseen clinical domains. The code is available at https://github.com/Ren-ling/ReGA.

</details>


### [52] [GuideGround: VLM-guided Semantic Understanding and Viewpoint-aware Reasoning for 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00518)
*Yiwen Wang,Yuyang Deng,Yihao Long,Xi Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GuideGround，一种基于VLM引导的3D视觉定位框架，通过VLM生成开放词汇的语义描述代替传统闭集分类任务，并保留各视角的定位假设、利用VLM跨候选视角进行验证，在ReferIt3D基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉定位方法依赖闭集对象分类作为辅助语义理解任务，泛化能力有限；同时多视角特征聚合会削弱视角特有的证据。作者观察到VLM天然具备开放词汇语义理解和全局场景感知能力，因而提出利用VLM对现有定位模型进行补充而非替代。

Method: GuideGround由两部分组成：（1）语义增强：用VLM生成的对象语义描述替代辅助的闭集分类任务；（2）视角推理：不直接聚合多视角特征，而是通过逐视角定位保留各视角的定位假设，并利用VLM在候选视角间进行显式验证。

Result: 在ReferIt3D基准上的大量实验表明，GuideGround一致优于先前的最先进方法。消融研究验证了语义理解和视角推理两个策略均有效。

Conclusion: GuideGround证明VLM可以作为任务特定定位模型的补充模块，分别从语义增强和视角假设验证两方面提升3D视觉定位性能，为VLM与专用模型协同提供了新思路。

Abstract: 3D visual grounding aims to localize the target object in a 3D scene from a natural language query, requiring both fine-grained semantic understanding and viewpoint-dependent spatial reasoning. Existing methods typically formulate semantic understanding as an auxiliary closed-set object classification task and rely on multi-view feature aggregation for viewpoint reasoning, limiting semantic generalization and weakening viewpoint-specific evidence. We observe that vision-language models naturally provide complementary capabilities through open-vocabulary semantic understanding and global scene perception. Based on this insight, we propose GuideGround, a VLM-guided framework that complements rather than replaces task-specific grounding models by leveraging VLMs for semantic enhancement and viewpoint-specific hypothesis verification. Specifically, we replace auxiliary closed-set object classification with VLM-generated object semantic descriptions to enhance semantic understanding. Meanwhile, instead of directly aggregating multi-view representations, we preserve viewpoint-specific grounding hypotheses through per-view grounding and explicitly verify them using VLMs across candidate viewpoints. Extensive experiments on the ReferIt3D benchmark demonstrate that GuideGround consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of both the proposed semantic understanding and viewpoint reasoning strategies.

</details>


### [53] [Unleashing the Power of Text: Text-Guided Flow Matching for Image Fusion under Complex Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00530)
*Axi Niu,Jieheng Li,Kang Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Jinqiu Sun,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TGFusion，一种文本引导的潜空间流匹配框架，通过将任务、退化和生成线索编码为结构化提示，并利用Prompt-conditioned Multi-stream Joint Flow Transformer实现文本与视觉特征的双向交互，统一解决红外-可见光图像融合中的退化抑制与跨模态融合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外-可见光图像融合在真实退化场景下具有挑战性，退化不仅导致观测图像中模态特异性信息丢失，还会阻碍融合过程。现有方法通常将固定的全局文本表示注入视觉特征，难以适应用空变化的退化、局部结构及热显著性信息，因此需要更灵活的自适应文本引导机制。

Method: 提出TGFusion，一个文本引导的潜空间流匹配框架。首先将任务、退化和生成线索编码为结构化提示；其次设计Prompt-conditioned Multi-stream Joint Flow Transformer，将文本作为独立语义流与融合、可见光、红外三个视觉流并行处理；通过联合注意力实现语义与视觉表征在token层面的双向交互和逐层更新，使退化语义动态引导可靠信息选择和融合潜变量生成。

Result: 在公开基准和复杂退化场景上的大量实验表明，TGFusion在感知质量、图像自然度、结构细节保留和红外显著性保持方面均达到优异或具有竞争力的性能，并且在多种单一及复合退化条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TGFusion通过文本动态引导机制有效统一了退化抑制和跨模态融合，验证了结构化文本先验在退化场景下图像融合任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Infrared-visible image fusion under realistic degradation scenarios is a challenging task, as degradations not only cause a loss of reliable modality-specific information in observed images but also hinder the fusion process. Recent studies indicate that text can provide prior information about degradation characteristics, complementing the limited evidence available from corrupted input images and facilitating fusion. However, existing methods typically inject fixed global text representations into visual features, making it difficult for textual guidance to adapt to spatially varying degradations, local structures, and thermal saliency. To this end, we propose TGFusion, a text-guided latent-space flow matching framework that unifies degradation suppression and cross-modal fusion. TGFusion encodes task, degradation, and generation cues into structured prompts. To fully exploit these priors, we design a Prompt-conditioned Multi-stream Joint Flow Transformer that represents text as an independent semantic stream alongside fusion, visible, and infrared streams. Joint attention enables token-level bidirectional interaction and layer-wise updating among semantic and visual representations, allowing degradation semantics to dynamically guide reliable information selection and fusion latent generation. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks and complex degradation scenarios demonstrate that TGFusion achieves superior or competitive performance in perceptual quality, image naturalness, structural-detail preservation, and infrared-saliency retention, while remaining robust across diverse single and compound degradations.

</details>


### [54] [DocPO: Advancing Document Policy Optimization via Tailored Step-Aware Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00536)
*Yunhao Wang,Binghong Wu,Zhenyu Huang,Jiacheng Shi,Shuo Huang,Tinghao Yu,Feng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Step-Aware Annealing (SAA)奖励锐化机制和DocPO文档策略优化框架，通过渐进增加奖励曲率来放大高精度区间中高分段样本间的细微质量差异，解决文档解析中编辑距离奖励在高准确率区间判别力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档解析的强化学习常依赖基于编辑距离的参考奖励，但在高准确率区间这些奖励判别力弱——接近正确的输出得分非常相似，难以提供有效学习信号，导致难以优化。

Method: 1) 提出SAA（Step-Aware Annealing），一种即插即用的奖励锐化机制，在训练中逐步增加奖励曲率，放大高分段样本间的细微质量差异，同时保持早期学习的稳定性。2) 基于SAA构建DocPO框架，为不同文档元素设计特定奖励：文本用归一化字符串编辑距离(NED)，表格用树编辑距离相似度(TEDS)，公式用混合Rubric+编辑距离奖励。

Result: 在OmniDocBench和DocElemHard基准上，SAA一致性地提升了GRPO风格强化学习在各文档元素上的性能，优于非退火奖励，且无需额外人工监督来构建奖励。

Conclusion: SAA通过奖励锐化机制有效解决了高准确率区间奖励判别力不足的问题，DocPO框架展示了在不同文档元素上自适应的奖励设计有效性，为文档解析的强化学习提供了无需额外人工标注的改进路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for document parsing often relies on reference-based rewards rooted in edit distance (e.g., tree edit distance), yet it remains hard to optimize in the high-accuracy regime because such rewards become weakly discriminative: near-correct outputs receive very similar scores, providing limited learning signal for hard cases. We propose Step-Aware Annealing (SAA), a plug-and-play reward sharpening mechanism that progressively increases reward curvature during training, amplifying subtle quality differences among high-scoring samples while preserving stability in early learning. Built on SAA, we introduce DocPO, a document policy optimization framework with element-specific, reference-based rewards anchored by edit-distance signals: normalized string edit distance (NED) for text, tree edit distance similarity (TEDS) for tables, and a hybrid Rubric+edit reward for formulas. Experiments on OmniDocBench and DocElemHard show that SAA consistently improves GRPO-style RL across document elements over non-annealed rewards, without requiring additional human supervision for reward construction.

</details>


### [55] [Hybrid-Domain Posterior Sampling for Inverse Problems via Latent Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00537)
*Hongjie Wu,Yiping Xie,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HDPS（混合域后验采样）框架，解决了潜在流模型在高保真逆问题中因自编码器"一阶流形盲区"而丢失高频信息的瓶颈。HDPS在像素空间执行Langevin动力学以精确吸收测量梯度，再通过优化式潜在对齐将结构修正投影回生成流形，实现了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在流模型虽然革新了压缩空间图像合成，但在高保真逆问题中的应用受到瓶颈限制。作者将其归因于预训练自编码器的一阶流形盲区：严重的解码器压缩导致雅可比矩阵秩亏，使得与流形正交的高频测量残差对潜在梯度不可见，即使解码器能够表示目标图像。

Method: 提出HDPS（混合域后验采样）解耦推理框架：首先将推断过程发散到像素空间，利用Langevin动力学吸收精确的正交测量梯度；随后通过优化式潜在对齐将这些结构修正投影回生成流形，在过滤像素空间伪影的同时避免直接编码导致的语义漂移。

Result: 在多种逆问题上的大量实验表明，HDPS建立了新的最先进水平，成功恢复了潜在域求解器固有丢失的高频结构精度。

Conclusion: HDPS通过像素与潜在域的协同解耦，有效克服了预训练自编码器的几何局限性，为高保真逆问题求解提供了新的范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Latent Flow Models have revolutionized compressed-space image synthesis, yet their application to high-fidelity inverse problems remains bottlenecked. In this paper, we trace this dilemma to a fundamental geometric limitation of pre-trained autoencoders, which we term \emph{First-Order Manifold Blindness}. Severe decoder compression (e.g., retaining only $\sim\!2\%$ of the original degrees of freedom) produces a rank-deficient Jacobian, rendering high-frequency measurement residuals in its orthogonal complement invisible to latent gradients even when the decoder can represent the target image. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose Hybrid-Domain Posterior Sampling (HDPS), a decoupled inference framework that disentangles physical measurement consistency from semantic prior modeling. HDPS diverges into the pixel space, leveraging Langevin dynamics to absorb precise orthogonal measurement gradients, and subsequently projects these structural corrections back onto the generative manifold. An optimization-based latent alignment is introduced to filter pixel-space artifacts while avoiding the semantic drift of direct encoding. Extensive experiments on diverse inverse problems demonstrate that HDPS establishes a new state-of-the-art, successfully recovering the high-frequency structural precision that latent-only solvers inherently discard. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/74587887/HDPS}{https://github.com/74587887/HDPS}.

</details>


### [56] [DiffuseAgent-MI: Distributionally-Grounded,Tool-Integrated Self-Evolving Agents for Faithful Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00540)
*An Lanji,Dawei Liu,Jin Li,Haoran Xu,Mei Chen,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffuseAgent-MI，一个基于KL最小能量模型的视觉-语言智能体，通过能量景观约束生成样本贴近内部表示，并结合验证器提供轨迹级忠实度奖励与修复机制，在多个基准上提升准确率最高5.1点，同时将互信息忠实度和人类可解释一致性提升两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具集成的视觉语言智能体在组合式和多步视觉推理上取得了显著进展，但其输出常存在不忠实问题——陈述的推理路径与实际产生答案的计算过程不一致，这在安全关键应用中会削弱可靠性。

Method: DiffuseAgent-MI是一个自进化智能体，其感知基础由基于特征单元的KL最小能量模型支配，提供视觉机制可解释性的分布视角。智能体学习一个能量景观，softly约束生成样本使其接近以选定可解释单元为条件的内在原分布，弥合解释与内部表示之间的差距。验证器提供轨迹级忠实度奖励，修复分支在验证器标记不忠实步骤时重新调节能量。

Result: 在GeoQA、SciVis、VQA-v2和内部多模态推理集上，DiffuseAgent-MI相比先前的自进化智能体准确率最高提升5.1点，同时互信息忠实度和人类可解释一致性均提升两倍以上。分析表明能量项和验证器互补：前者保证分布级忠实度，后者保证轨迹级忠实度，两者结合才能同时弥合两方面的差距。

Conclusion: 能量模型与验证器的组合是同时实现分布级和轨迹级忠实度的必要条件，两者互补而非替代，共同构建了更可靠、可解释的多模态推理智能体。

Abstract: Tool-integrated vision-language agents have made remarkable progress on compositional and multi-step visual reasoning. Yet their outputs frequently exhibit unfaithfulness: the stated reasoning path diverges from the computation that actually produced the answer, undermining reliability in safety-critical applications. We present DiffuseAgent-MI, a self-evolving agent whose perceptual grounding is governed by a KL-minimal energy model over feature units, providing a distributional view of visual mechanistic interpretability. The agent learns an energy landscape that softly constrains generated samples to lie near the native prior conditioned on the chosen interpretable unit, closing the gap between the explanation and the internal representation. A verifier then supplies trajectory-level faithfulness rewards, and a repair branch re-conditions the energy when the verifier flags an unfaithful step. On GeoQA, SciVis, VQA-v2 and an in-house multimodal reasoning set, DiffuseAgent-MI improves accuracy by up to 5.1 points over prior self-evolving agents while more than doubling mutual-information faithfulness and human-interpretability agreement. Our analysis shows the energy term and the verifier are complementary: the former guarantees distributional faithfulness, the latter trajectory-level faithfulness, and only their combination closes both gaps.

</details>


### [57] [Zero-Cost Virtual RNA: Approximating Immunotherapy Signatures via Cross-Modal WSI Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00544)
*Sigrid Vila-Bagaria,Mar Teixidó,Miquel Piñol,Felip Vilardell,Robert Montal,Veronica Vilaplana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VITA是一种基于H&E切片的虚拟转录组近似方法，通过训练时将H&E和RNA映射到联合潜在空间，推理时仅需标准病理切片即可检索形态相似的历史病例并近似连续RNA特征，以低成本预测胃腺癌的"炎症性"免疫表型及免疫治疗反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的10基因RNA签名虽能预测胃腺癌免疫治疗反应，但成本高昂；而基于深度学习直接在H&E切片上分类的方法将连续的RNA数据二元化，过度简化并引入标签噪声，需要一种可扩展且保留连续表型信息的替代方案。

Method: 提出VITA框架，在训练阶段将H&E组织切片和RNA转录组数据对齐到联合潜在空间；在推理阶段仅需H&E切片，通过检索形态学上相似的历史病例来近似连续的RNA签名。

Result: VITA实现了0.72的分类准确率和0.66的Spearman相关系数，证明其能够在保留连续表型谱系的同时，有效预测免疫治疗相关表型。

Conclusion: VITA作为一种经济高效的"虚拟转录组学"预筛查工具，无需基因组测序即可提供连续的肿瘤免疫表型评估，为胃癌免疫治疗反应预测提供了可扩展的临床解决方案。

Abstract: Identifying the ``Inflamed'' immunophenotype in Gastric Adenocarcinoma predicts immunotherapy response but requires an expensive 10-gene RNA signature. While deep learning on standard H\&E slides offers a scalable alternative, conventional binary classifiers oversimplify continuous RNA data and introduce label noise. To resolve this, we propose VITA (VIrtual Transcriptomic Approximation). By aligning H\&E and RNA into a joint latent space during training, VITA requires only standard H\&E at inference to retrieve morphologically similar historical cases and approximate the continuous RNA signature. Achieving 0.72 classification accuracy and a 0.66 Spearman correlation, VITA provides a cost-effective ``virtual transcriptomics'' pre-screening tool that preserves the continuous phenotypic spectrum without requiring genomic sequencing.

</details>


### [58] [DrawAI: Agentic Benchmark and Workflow for Making Raster Images Editable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00548)
*Pu Cao,Qingye Kong,Xuedan Yin,Xuekun Zhao,Rupeng Yan,Qing Song,Yao Zhang,Lu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像到可编辑重建任务，并引入DrawAI基准（DrawAI-Bench）和重建工作流（DrawAI-Flow），通过39项混合标准评估保真度与可编辑性，系统评估13个模型×5种agent框架，证明DrawAI-Flow能稳定提升可编辑结构质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型和多模态agent虽能产出高质量视觉内容，但栅格输出将语义内容和关系扁平化为像素，导致用户无法检查、修改、重排或重用单个组件，难以在实际视觉创作场景中直接使用。

Method: 提出两阶段agent工作流DrawAI-Flow：解析agent将提取的元素证据转化为显式重建计划，重建agent通过代码-渲染-验证-修改的迭代循环将计划实现为可执行图形代码。同时构建DrawAI-Bench基准，覆盖科学图表、演示幻灯片、海报和图表，包含真实与AI生成图像，采用39项混合评估标准（确定性规则指标+资产特定视觉语言rubric）衡量保真度和可编辑性。

Result: 在DrawAI-Bench上对13个模型和5种agent框架进行系统评估，结果显示重建质量和成本在不同模型-框架配置间差异显著，而DrawAI-Flow一致性地提升了可编辑结构的质量。

Conclusion: 图像到可编辑重建是一个兼具挑战性和实用性的任务，保真度与可编辑性之间存在权衡；DrawAI-Bench提供了可靠的评估手段，DrawAI-Flow作为两阶段agent工作流能有效提升重建的可编辑结构，为后续研究提供重要参考。

Abstract: Recent image-generation models and multimodal agents can produce high-quality visuals for increasingly complex visual communication tasks. Yet their raster outputs remain difficult to use directly because meaningful content and relationships are flattened into pixels, preventing users from inspecting, modifying, rearranging, or reusing individual components. We formulate image-to-editable reconstruction, which recovers a structured, directly manipulable artifact from a raster image while preserving its visual and semantic content. The central challenge is to jointly satisfy Fidelity and Editability, which often trade off in practice. To study this task, we introduce DrawAI, comprising an agentic benchmark, DrawAI-Bench, and a reconstruction workflow, DrawAI-Flow. DrawAI-Bench spans scientific figures, presentation slides, posters, and diagrams, combining real and AI-generated images to reflect practical visual-creation scenarios. It evaluates Fidelity and Editability through a hybrid protocol of 39 criteria: deterministic rule-based metrics measure properties with direct correspondences, while asset-specific vision-language rubrics capture semantic and perceptual qualities for which exact matching is misleading. Besides, we propose DrawAI-Flow, a two-stage agentic workflow in which a Parser Agent turns extracted elements evidence into an explicit reconstruction plan, and a Reconstruction Agent realizes the plan as executable graphics code through an iterative code-render-validate-revise loop. On DrawAI-Bench, we systematically evaluate thirteen models across five agent harnesses to study the effects of model capability, harness choice, and workflow design. The results show that reconstruction quality and costs vary substantially across model-harness configurations, while DrawAI-Flow consistently improves editable structure.

</details>


### [59] [Test-Time Curriculum for Open-Set AIGC Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00559)
*Yiqian Zhang,Zheyuan Gu,Xiangzhao Hao,Zefeng Zhang,Jingjia Mao,Jiahao Hu,Jiaxu Miao,Jun Yu,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种测试时自适应框架TTC（Test-Time Curriculum），通过基于课程的自训练方法，让AIGC图像检测器在部署后能够适应新出现的生成模型带来的分布偏移，无需重新训练即可提升开放场景下的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC图像检测方法虽然通过更好的表示或训练数据构造改进了跨生成器的泛化能力，但它们通常采用静态的"一次训练、永久部署"模式，无法在部署后适应新出现的、更强的生成模型所带来的分布偏移。

Method: TTC框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于课程的自训练：从未标注测试数据中从高置信度伪标签样本开始，逐步引入更难但更有信息量的样本；(2) 类别平衡选择：在生成器偏移下减少有偏更新；(3) 跨尺度伪标签精炼：通过多分辨率聚合互补证据提升伪标签质量，推理时使用noisy-or融合增强最终预测。此外还构建了包含3100张真实图像和12.4万张来自40个先进文生图模型的生成图像的AIGCGuard基准。

Result: 在五个基准上的大量实验表明，TTC在各种未见生成器偏移场景下显著提升了整体检测性能，验证了该框架在开放集生成图像检测中的有效性。

Conclusion: TTC建立了一个实用且有效的测试时自适应框架，使AIGC图像检测器能够在部署后持续适应新出现的生成模型，为开放环境中的生成内容检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors deployed in open-world environments inevitably face distribution shifts as new and stronger generative models continue to emerge. Although existing methods improve cross-generator generalization through better representations or training data construction, they typically follow a static train-once-and-deploy paradigm and cannot adapt after deployment. In this work, we study open-set AIGC image detection from a test-time adaptation perspective. We propose Test-Time Curriculum (TTC), a simple and model-agnostic framework that adapts a detector on unlabeled test data through curriculum-based self-training. TTC starts from highly reliable pseudo-labeled samples and progressively incorporates harder yet informative cases, while enforcing class-balanced selection to reduce biased updates under generator shift. To further improve pseudo-label quality, we introduce Cross-Scale Pseudo-Label Refinement, which aggregates complementary evidence across multiple resolutions for more reliable adaptation, and applies noisy-or fusion at inference to strengthen final predictions. In addition, we construct AIGCGuard, a new benchmark containing 3,100 representative real images and 124,000 generated images from 40 of the most advanced open-source and proprietary text-to-image models. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks show that TTC substantially improves overall detection performance under diverse unseen-generator shifts, establishing a practical and effective test-time adaptation framework for open-set generated image detection.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Token-Level Cross-Entropy: Fréchet Distributional Post-Training for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00562)
*Jinhua Zhang,Yisong Lin,Wei Long,Shuhang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FD-loss后训练方法，通过表示空间中的Fréchet距离作为唯一目标，对预训练的自回归图像生成器进行微调，解决训练目标与评估指标不一致的问题，在ImageNet 256×256上平均降低FID 41.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归图像生成器通过教师强制下的token级交叉熵预训练，但评估关注生成图像的分布质量，存在目标不匹配（分类错误具有不同的图像级后果）和上下文不匹配（推理时基于模型自生成的历史序列）两大问题。

Method: 采用双通道方案：第一通道在模型原生推理配置下通过无梯度生成构建分离的rollout上下文；第二通道使用概率级直通估计器（STE）进行可微分重放，前向传播保持硬argmax解码，同时通过温度缩放概率传播图像级梯度。仅更新生成器，tokenizer和特征提取器保持冻结。

Result: 在四个生成器家族的八个配置上，FD-loss后训练平均降低FID和FD_r6分别达41.4%和52.0%，最强的FID结果从2.42提升至1.43，且未增加参数量或推理步骤。

Conclusion: FD-loss后训练有效弥合了训练与评估目标之间的差距，以表示空间Fréchet距离作为唯一优化目标，无需额外参数或推理成本即可显著提升自回归图像生成器的分布质量。

Abstract: Autoregressive image generators are commonly pretrained with token-level cross-entropy under teacher forcing, yet evaluated by the distributional quality of decoded images. This creates an objective mismatch, because categorical errors have unequal image-level consequences, and a context mismatch, because inference conditions on model-generated histories. We introduce FD-loss post-training, which adapts a pretrained discrete generator using representation-space Fréchet distance as the sole objective. A dual-pass scheme first constructs detached rollout contexts through gradient-free generation under the model's native inference configuration, then performs differentiable replay with a probability-level straight-through estimator (STE) that preserves hard argmax decoding in the forward pass while propagating image-level gradients through temperature-scaled probabilities. Only the generator is updated, while the tokenizer and feature extractors remain frozen. Across eight completed configurations from four generator families on class-conditional ImageNet at $256\times256$, FD-loss post-training reduces FID and $\mathrm{FD}_{r6}$ by 41.4% and 52.0% on average. The strongest FID result improves from 2.42 to 1.43 without adding parameters or inference steps.

</details>


### [61] [Relax Within, Balance Across: Geometry-Guided Load Balancing for Vision-Language Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00574)
*Ziang Wu,Peng Jin,Qishen Yin,Munan Ning,Hao Li,Peizhen Zhang,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReBA（Relax Within, Balance Across），一种改进视觉-语言MoE批次负载均衡的方法。针对标准辅助损失（Std-Aux）在不同图像/文本token混合比例下负载失衡的问题，ReBA基于图像与文本token的结构性差异，分别设置独立的文本负载项和按图像实例等权路由的负载项，在保持任务精度的同时大幅降低各类输入下的负载不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言MoE批次中图像与文本token数量随图像分辨率、数量、平铺和提示长度而剧烈变化。标准的token级Switch辅助损失（Std-Aux）只对混合负载做均衡，当图像和文本负载误差正负相消时会出现盲区；实际验证中同一训练好的路由在不同图像分辨率下负载不均衡可变化五倍以上，亟需针对性修复。

Method: 首先在固定图像和文本负载分布的条件下，推导出负载曲线随token混合比例变化的精确表达式，发现图像-文本负载差距决定了对token混合比例的敏感性。随后分析路由器输入结构，发现图像和文本token占据不同区域，视觉token按来源图像强聚类，据此提出ReBA：在模态边界上设置独立的图像与文本负载项（Balance Across），并在图像内部允许token自由分配（Relax Within），对每张图像使用等权路由实例。

Result: 在四种split backbone上，ReBA在所有报告的基准输入上降低了负载，同时保持与Std-Aux相当的平均任务精度；在测试的token混合范围内降低了平均负载，并在分辨率和平铺变化下降低了最坏情况的物理负载。

Conclusion: ReBA通过解耦图像和文本负载项并利用视觉token的图像分组结构，有效解决视觉-语言MoE中token混合比例变化导致的负载失衡问题，且不牺牲任务精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language MoE batches contain different numbers of image and text tokens. Image resolution, image count, tiling, and prompt length all change this token mix. We call the standard token-level Switch auxiliary loss Std-Aux. Std-Aux balances only the mixed load, so large image and text load errors can cancel at one mix. On our main model, the same trained router shows more than a fivefold change in load imbalance across image resolutions. We hold the image and text load profiles fixed and derive the exact load curve as the token mix varies. The image-text load gap controls sensitivity to the token mix. Physical preprocessing can also change the conditional profiles. The fixed-profile law excludes such changes. To design a remedy, we examine the router input structure. Image and text occupy distinct regions, while visual tokens group strongly by source image. The modality boundary motivates separate image and text terms. The image boundary motivates one equal-weight routing instance per image. ReBA, or Relax Within, Balance Across, implements both choices. Across four split backbones, ReBA lowers load on every reported benchmark input while keeping mean task accuracy comparable to Std-Aux. ReBA also lowers average load over the tested range and worst physical load under resolution and tiling shifts. Code is available at https://github.com/ZiangWu-77/ReBA.

</details>


### [62] [Element-Aware Group Learning for E-Commerce Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00584)
*Jingtong Chen,Jiahui Wang,Xue Zhao,ShaoGuo Liu,Minghao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EAGLE-GRPO方法，将组相对策略优化(GRPO)中的粗粒度奖励分解到预定义的图像设计元素层面，通过核岭回归闭式解实现无需额外采样或评论模型的元素级信用分配，从而提升电商图像生成提示词的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电商创意场景中，视觉语言模型生成的图像编辑提示词质量成为关键瓶颈。GRPO虽适用于结果级奖励优化，但仅在完整提示词层面分配信用，无法捕捉图像质量对构图、背景、卖点呈现等具体设计元素的依赖，现有细粒度信用分配方法又需要步骤级监督或训练额外的评论模型。

Method: 提出EAGLE-GRPO框架：将围绕组的奖励按预定义元素进行分解，将元素级信用分配建模为核岭回归问题并推导出闭式解，从而无需额外rollout或独立的信用分配模型，即可获得可解释的逐元素优势并实现更精确的策略更新。

Result: 实验表明，EAGLE-GRPO在更多训练步骤上持续保持性能增益后才趋于平稳，生成的提示词能够产生比具有竞争力的VLM提示词写作基线更高质量的电商图像。

Conclusion: EAGLE-GRPO通过元素感知的信用分配有效解决了GRPO在电商图像提示词生成中的粗粒度信用分配问题，在无需额外计算开销的情况下实现更优的提示词质量和更稳定的训练过程。

Abstract: Recent advances in image generation and editing have made prompt quality a key bottleneck for e-commerce creatives. Vision-language models (VLMs) can generate image-editing prompts from product images and metadata, but further improving their prompt-writing capabilities requires post-training with feedback from the generated images. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a natural framework for such outcome-level reward optimization. However, it assigns credit only at the full-prompt level, even though image quality often depends on specific design elements such as composition, background, and the presentation of selling points. Existing fine-grained credit assignment methods typically require step-level supervision or learned critics. To address this, we propose EAGLE-GRPO (Element-Aware Group Learning for E-Commerce Image Generation), which decomposes the group-centered reward over predefined elements. We cast element-level credit assignment as a kernel ridge regression problem and derive a closed-form solution, without additional rollouts or separate credit-assignment models. This yields interpretable per-element advantages and more precise policy updates. Experiments show that EAGLE-GRPO sustains performance gains over more training steps before plateauing and generates prompts that produce higher-quality e-commerce images than competitive VLM prompt-writing baselines.

</details>


### [63] [Representation Transfer of Foundation Models for Ultra-Widefield Retinal Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00586)
*Mingya Alexa Gong,Da Ma,Lovre Antonio Budimir,Ivana Matovinovic,Sven Loncaric,Myeong Jin Ju,Yukun Zhou,Siegfried K. Wagner,Pearse A. Keane,Marinko V. Sarunic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同预训练策略（监督学习、MAE、自蒸馏）的Vision Transformer编码器在超广角视网膜影像弱监督分类任务中的表示迁移能力，发现预训练目标显著影响MIL框架中的patch聚合行为与下游性能，其中大规模预训练的DINOv3表现最佳（五类糖尿病视网膜病变分级QWK达0.863），且部分微调可缩小MAE的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型已广泛作为医学影像的特征提取器，但不同预训练策略如何影响学到的表示向弱监督眼科影像任务的迁移能力，目前了解甚少。作者希望在超广角视网膜影像上系统性地回答这一问题。

Method: 在超广角视网膜影像上，采用基于patch的多实例学习（MIL）框架进行疾病分类。以Vision Transformer编码器为特征提取器，分别使用监督学习、掩码自编码器（MAE）和自蒸馏目标进行预训练，保持下游聚合架构不变，进行受控对比实验，并辅以注意力分析和部分微调实验。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k预训练的ViT-B编码器受控对比中，预训练目标显著影响冻结表示迁移效果，监督学习和自蒸馏模型优于MAE；大规模预训练的DINOv3取得最强整体性能（五类DR分级QWK=0.863，与DINOv1相当）；注意力分析揭示不同预训练表示对应不同的patch聚合行为；部分微调可大幅缩小MAE的性能差距。

Conclusion: 预训练策略同时影响表示的可迁移性和MIL框架中patch级证据的聚合方式，进而导致下游分类性能的差异。该发现为医学影像领域预训练模型选择与下游聚合架构设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of foundation models as feature extractors for medical imaging, relatively little is understood about how different pretraining strategies influence the transferability of learned representations to weakly supervised ophthalmic imaging tasks. We investigate this question in ultra-widefield (UWF) retinal imaging by evaluating foundation model representations within a patch-based multiple instance learning (MIL) framework for disease classification on UWF images. We compare Vision Transformer encoders pretrained with supervised, Masked Autoencoder (MAE), and self-distillation objectives, while keeping the downstream aggregation architecture unchanged. Within a controlled comparison of ViT-B encoders pretrained on ImageNet-1k, the choice of pretraining objective substantially influenced frozen representation transfer, with supervised and self-distillation-based models outperforming MAE. A contemporary DINOv3 model pretrained at a larger scale achieved the strongest overall performance, with a quadratic weighted kappa of 0.863 for five-class diabetic retinopathy grading, comparable with DINOv1. Attention analysis further revealed distinct patch-aggregation behaviours associated with the different pretrained representations, while partial fine-tuning substantially reduced the performance gap for MAE. These findings suggest that pretraining strategy influences both representation transferability and the subsequent aggregation of patch-level evidence within MIL, resulting in differences in downstream classification performance.

</details>


### [64] [InstancePin: Instance-Addressable Layout-to-Image Diffusion via Coordinate Pinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00588)
*Chaoyue Wu,Yunfei Zhang,Si Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstancePin是一个实例可寻址的布局到图像扩散框架，通过为每个对象实例引入显式坐标锚点来解决密集城市场景中同类别对象之间特征混淆的问题，在Cityscapes上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的布局到图像扩散模型虽然通过语义分割图实现了类别级别的语义控制，但并不具备实例可寻址性——同一类别中多个相邻对象被当作共享语义区域，导致边界模糊、外观平均化和特征混淆。这在城市场景合成中尤其明显，因为密集的行人和车辆需要精细的实例分离同时保持全局场景一致性。

Method: InstancePin通过三个核心组件实现实例级控制：(1) 独立的实例感知适配器，保留预训练骨干的类别级生成先验同时学习实例特定的空间控制；(2) 坐标锚定机制，每个实例的中心坐标经傅里叶特征编码后投影为坐标令牌，通过坐标钉扎注意力与潜在图像特征查询交互；(3) 实例掩码引导的融合模块，将骨干特征路由到非实例区域、适配器特征路由到实例区域，实现局部实例细化而不牺牲全局语义保真度。此外，还通过坐标注意力图与实例区域的一致性监督来确保坐标令牌激活对应对象区域。

Result: 在Cityscapes数据集上的大量实验表明，InstancePin有效缓解了密集布局中的实例纠缠问题，同时在图像保真度和语义一致性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: InstancePin通过显式坐标锚定和实例感知适配器的设计，成功地将预训练类别级布局扩散模型扩展为实例可寻址的生成框架，在不牺牲类别级先验的前提下实现了细粒度的实例级空间控制，为密集城市场景合成提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Layout-to-image diffusion models have achieved impressive semantic controllability by conditioning generation on category-level segmentation maps. However, such category-aligned control is not necessarily instance-addressable: multiple nearby objects from the same category are often treated as a shared semantic region, leading to ambiguous boundaries, averaged appearances, and feature confusion among instances. This limitation is particularly evident in urban scene synthesis, where small and crowded pedestrians or vehicles require fine-grained instance separation while preserving global scene consistency. In this paper, we propose InstancePin, an instance-addressable layout-to-image diffusion framework that pins each object instance with an explicit coordinate anchor. Instead of directly injecting instance masks into the pretrained backbone, InstancePin introduces an independent instance-aware adapter to preserve the category-level generation prior while learning instance-specific spatial control. For each instance, its center coordinate is encoded with Fourier features and projected into a coordinate token, which serves as a spatial anchor queried by latent image features through coordinate pinning attention. To make these anchors spatially meaningful, we further supervise the coordinate attention maps with instance regions, encouraging each coordinate token to activate its corresponding object area. Finally, an instance-mask guided fusion module routes pretrained backbone features to non-instance regions and adapter features to instance regions, enabling local instance refinement without sacrificing global semantic fidelity. Extensive experiments on Cityscapes demonstrate that InstancePin mitigates instance entanglement in dense layouts and improves both image fidelity and semantic consistency.

</details>


### [65] [Diagnosing Under-Development of Irreversible Processes in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00617)
*Jian Xu,Yanning Wu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视频生成模型是否尊重不可逆物理属性（如冰融化、纸张燃烧），发现现有逆转度量具有零退化性（纯噪声即可得0.50违规率）。作者提出双部分协议（方向性属性相关性和停滞率）替代逆转测量，发现七个文生视频模型在不可逆属性上几乎零进展、92-100%停滞，与真实视频（ρ=+0.40, 35%静态）形成鲜明对比。此外，事后读出引导可被破解，而构造性单调性约束可消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理世界充满不可逆过程（冰融化不复冻结、纸燃烧不复原），但视频生成模型是否尊重这些不可逆属性尚不清楚。关键难点在于逆转难以可靠测量，现有指标噪音即达标。作者旨在建立经过零检验的评估协议，并测试生成模型在不可逆属性上的忠实度。

Method: 提出替代逆转评估的双部分协议：(1) 进展度量——方向性属性相关性（progress），(2) 停滞率（stasis rate）。对七个文生视频模型与真实参考视频进行比较，额外采用9名标注者进行人工验证。此外检验事后读出引导的可游戏性，并探索构造性单调性约束的替代方案。

Result: 真实参考视频在不可逆属性上展现出显著进展（ρ=+0.40，35%静态），而所有生成模型均接近零进展且92-100%停滞。人工评分证实真实视频（2.75/4）远优于生成视频（0.99/4）。实证表明生成器的真正缺陷是"发展不足"而非"逆转"。另发现事后读出引导可被利用，构造性方法可消除该问题。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型在不可逆物理属性上的可靠缺陷是发展不足——生成视频几乎不推动不可逆属性进展，而非错误地逆转它们。评估协议须通过零假设检验，且增强不可逆属性遵循的方向可通过构造性单调性约束实现，而非事后读出引导。

Abstract: Many physical attributes are \emph{irreversible}: ice melts but does not re-freeze, paper chars but does not un-burn. Do video generators respect this? We show the question is hard to measure, and that what can be measured reliably is \emph{development} rather than reversal. Metrics of local reversal are null-degenerate: a per-clip violation rate scores $0.50$ on pure noise, and a variance-normalized reversal residual sits at its noise ceiling. What survives null-testing is a two-part protocol: progress (a directional attribute correlation) and a stasis rate. Under this protocol, generated video separates cleanly from real footage, and the gap is human-validated. Across seven text-to-video models, real reference footage advances ($ρ{=}{+}0.40$, $35\%$ static) while every generator shows near-zero progress and $92$--$100\%$ stasis; nine annotators rate real footage far above generated ($2.75$ vs.\ $0.99$ on a $0$--$4$ scale). The reliable finding is \emph{under-development}: generators barely advance irreversible attributes rather than reversing them. As a complementary mechanism, we show that post-hoc readout guidance is gameable, whereas enforcing monotonicity by construction in a disentangled attribute latent removes the gameable readout, validated in controlled and semi-synthetic settings.

</details>


### [66] [Where Does Generative Difficulty Reside? An Empirical Study of Target Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00626)
*Marcel Plocher,Bernhard Schölkopf,Andreas Geiger,Gege Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续掩码生成器中不同目标表示（原始像素、SD-VAE潜变量、DINOv2和MAE特征）对生成性能的影响，发现这些表示在优化、推理和分布控制上存在显著差异，且压缩率、重构保真度、token维度和语义聚类均不能单独预测生成行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标表示通常被视为可互换的接口，但这一假设在连续掩码生成器中值得质疑——因为这些生成器结合了上下文推断与逐token条件建模，不同表示空间可能从根本上影响生成过程。

Method: 在统一的掩码自回归整流流模型框架下，对四种目标表示（原始像素、SD-VAE潜变量、DINOv2特征和MAE表示自编码器特征）进行系统比较。在共享的ImageNet训练预算下，分别评估每种表示的优化收敛速度、推理配置需求、分类器无关引导响应以及精确率-召回率权衡。

Result: DINOv2收敛最快但需要更宽的局部去噪器和直接上下文融合；像素优化显著更慢且需要不同的预测、掩码和引导配置；MAE重构更忠实且具有清晰的语义聚类，但生成质量明显不如DINOv2。不同表示对分类器无关引导的响应也各不相同，占据不同的精确率-召回率权衡。

Conclusion: 目标表示的选择会重新分配上下文建模、逐token去噪和推理时分布控制之间的难度，而不是简单的接口替换。压缩率、重构保真度、token维度和可见语义聚类均不能单独预测生成行为，说明了目标空间在生成模型设计中的核心地位。

Abstract: The target representation defines the distribution an image generator must learn, yet it is often treated as an interchangeable interface. This assumption is particularly questionable for continuous masked generators, which combine contextual inference from visible tokens with conditional modeling of each missing token. We study raw pixels, SD-VAE latents and DINOv2 as well as MAE representation-autoencoder features within a unified masked autoregressive rectified-flow model. Under a shared ImageNet training budget, these spaces exhibit distinct optimization and inference regimes. DINOv2 converges fastest in both iterations and computation but benefits strongly from a wider local denoiser and direct context fusion. Pixels optimize substantially more slowly and require a different prediction, masking, and guidance configuration. MAE reconstructs images more faithfully and exhibits clear semantic clustering, yet produces generations substantially worse than DINOv2. The representations also respond differently to classifier-free guidance and occupy distinct precision-recall trade-offs. Together, our results show that compression, reconstruction fidelity, token dimensionality, and visible semantic clustering do not individually predict generative behavior. Instead, target representations redistribute difficulty across contextual modeling, per-token denoising, and inference-time distributional control.

</details>


### [67] [WiFuse: An Attention Mechanism for Human Activity Recognition using Fused CSI Amplitude and Delay-Doppler Channel Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00642)
*Alison M. Fernandes,Hermes I. Del Monego,Bruno S. Chang,Anelise Munaretto,Hélder M. Fontes,Rui L. Campos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WiFuse框架，一种双流CSI（信道状态信息）人体活动识别框架，将去噪的时域幅度变化与基于2D-FFT的Delay-Doppler运动表示相结合，输入混合ResNet-TCN神经网络架构（配备通道和时空注意力机制），并采用两阶段解耦迁移学习策略。在XRF55和Wi-MIR两个公共数据集上分别达到95.28%和98.20%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wi-Fi感知在人体活动识别中具有隐私保护和非侵入性等优势，但环境特征（如反射表面、硬件偏移等物理损伤）会严重影响神经网络识别性能，导致模型精度显著下降。现有方法在多径传播、噪声和干扰等不利条件下鲁棒性不足，需要一种能够克服这些环境退化因素的识别框架。

Method: WiFuse框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 双流CSI表示：将去噪的时域幅度变化与由净化后信道相位经2D-FFT得到的Delay-Doppler运动表示配对；(2) 混合ResNet-TCN神经网络架构：配备通道和时空注意力机制，ResNet提取空间-频谱特征，TCN建模长程时间依赖；(3) 两阶段解耦迁移学习策略：用于改善优化稳定性和特征复用。

Result: 在XRF55数据集四个环境中达到最高95.28%的总准确率，在多人Wi-MIR数据集上达到98.20%的准确率。实验包括与最先进方法的比较、替代混合架构的对比、消融研究以及跨数据集和域适应评估。

Conclusion: 将幅度和Delay-Doppler表示结合在双流策略中，并通过迁移学习增强，能够在类别重叠、多径传播、噪声和干扰等通常导致深度神经网络性能退化的条件下提高识别性能。

Abstract: Recently, Wi-Fi sensing has played a significant role in Human Activity Recognition (HAR), as it enables the detection of various activities using only Wi-Fi signals, ensuring privacy and remaining non-intrusive for the user. However, environmental characteristics such as reflective surfaces, hardware offsets, and other physical impairments affect recognition by the neural network, subsequently causing errors and significantly reducing model accuracy. To overcome this problem we present the WiFuse framework, a dual-stream Channel State Information (CSI) framework for human activity recognition (HAR) that pairs denoised time-domain amplitude variations with 2D-FFT-derived Delay-Doppler motion representations computed from the sanitized channel phase. The fused representation feeds a hybrid ResNet-Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) neural architecture augmented with channel and spatio-temporal attention, where the ResNet extracts spatial-spectral features and the TCN models long-range temporal dependencies; a decoupled two-stage transfer learning strategy is employed to improve optimization stability and feature reuse. We conduct extensive experiments on two public datasets, including comparisons against state-of-the-art methods and alternative hybrid architectures, ablation studies, and cross-dataset and domain-adaptation evaluations. The proposed framework reaches an overall accuracy of up to 95.28% across the four environments of the XRF55 dataset and up to 98.20% on the multi-user Wi-MIR dataset. Overall, the results indicate that combining amplitude and Delay-Doppler representations within a dual-stream strategy, enhanced by transfer learning, improves recognition performance under conditions that typically degrade deep neural networks, such as class overlap, multipath propagation, noise, and interference.

</details>


### [68] [PixelSR: Efficient Screen Content Super-Resolution via Pixel Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00646)
*Zhiheng Li,Lei Chen,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 像素SR（PixelSR）是一种面向屏幕内容超分辨率的简单有效方法，通过像素分箱的内容注意力提升性能，并利用屏幕内容的自重复性和冗余性，将像素分类为唯一、重复和背景三类来加速推理，实现更高质量的SR结果和更短的推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕内容图像由文本和图形组成，含有大量尖锐但重复的结构。现有屏幕内容超分辨率方法未能充分利用这些特殊特性，导致模型性能和推理速度仍有较大提升空间。

Method: 在训练阶段，通过像素分箱将像素分类到内容相关组中，从每组内聚合像素特征计算内容注意力，为每个像素引入内容相关且非局部的感受野。在测试阶段，利用屏幕内容的自重复性和冗余性，将高分辨率像素分为唯一像素、重复像素和背景像素三类：唯一像素通过常规网络处理并缓存预测结果到查询表；重复像素直接查表获取结果；背景像素用最近邻算法生成，从而加速推理。

Result: 实验表明PixelSR在屏幕内容超分辨率任务上达到了最先进的性能，同时推理时间更短。

Conclusion: PixelSR证明了充分利用屏幕内容的自身特性（像素分箱注意力提升质量，查表加速提升速度）能够在性能与速度上同时超越现有方法。

Abstract: Screen content images are generally composed of texts and graphics. Compared to natural images, these man-made images contain a large quantity of sharp but repetitive structures. However, existing works in screen content super-resolution underutilize the special characteristics of screen content, leaving a large room to improve model performance and speed up. In this paper, we propose PixelSR, a simple yet effective method to improve super-resolution performance but with faster inference speed. To improve model performance, we classify pixels via pixel binning to compute content attention in the training phase. Specifically, after binning pixels into content-dependent groups, content attention is aggregated from pixel features within each group to introduce a content-dependent and non-local receptive field for every pixel. In the testing phase, we utilize the properties of self-repetitiveness and redundancy in screen content to speed up inference without the loss of model performance. We divide targeted high-resolution pixels into three types, which are unique pixels, repeated pixels, and background pixels for each test image. We conduct conventional network processing on unique pixels and cache their predictions in the on-the-fly lookup table. For repeated pixels which have appeared in unique pixels, we directly retrieve prediction results from the lookup table without network processing. For background pixels, we use the nearest neighbor algorithm to generate high-resolution pixels. The on-the-fly lookup table is cleaned and repeats the procedure above for the next test image. Experiments show our PixelSR achieves state-of-the-art performance with shorter inference time in screen content super-resolution.

</details>


### [69] [Geometry-guided Emotion Modulation for Controllable and Photorealistic Emotional Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00663)
*Chenggong Hu,Shaoyin Ma,Yi Wang,Li Sun,Mingli Song,Jie Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GemTalk框架，结合隐式表示的语义丰富性与显式几何先验的结构精度，实现音频驱动的情感说话人脸生成，在照片真实感和面部情感动态方面达到先进性能。


<details>
