The proposed framework aligns with expert annotations and intraoperative surgeon gaze, and reduces surgeon cognitive workload during real-world evaluations using subjective, physiological, and behavioral measures.

</details>


### [273] [EchoCache: Energy-Guided Cross-Modal Caching for Efficient Audio-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02474)
*Jiayu Chen,Xiaoyu Wu,Rongshan Gao,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Xinhao Sun,Hailong Zou,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoCache是一个基于能量引导的跨模态缓存框架，用于加速音频驱动视频生成（A2V）的扩散模型推理过程，通过利用音频时频能量作为显著性锚点指导缓存更新，并引入动态时间步长-潜在缓存机制，在保持生成质量和音视频一致性的同时实现2.46倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频驱动视频生成（A2V）虽然取得了进展，但扩散模型的迭代去噪过程导致推理成本高昂。现有缓存方法主要利用视觉特征的时间冗余，忽略了A2V中音频对视觉生成的跨模态对齐特性，存在时间语义错配和计算存储错配两个层面的问题。

Method: 提出EchoCache框架，包含两个核心组件：1）利用音频时频能量作为显著性锚点，引导潜在级别的缓存更新；2）引入动态时间步长-潜在缓存机制，并结合量化缓存管理，实现效率和内存的联合优化。

Result: 在主流A2V模型上的大量实验表明，EchoCache一致地改善了延迟-质量权衡，同时保持生成质量和音视频一致性。在Wan2.2-S2V模型的EMTD基准上，实现了2.46倍加速并达到最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: EchoCache通过能量引导的跨模态缓存策略有效解决了A2V生成中现有缓存方法的错配问题，在保持质量的前提下显著提升了推理效率，为音频驱动视频生成的实用化部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Audio-driven video generation (A2V) has achieved promising progress in synthesizing temporally coherent and audio-visually aligned videos, yet its inference remains expensive due to the iterative denoising process of diffusion models. Existing caching methods mainly exploit temporal redundancy in visual features while overlooking the cross-modal alignment of A2V, where audio drives visual generation with highly non-uniform temporal importance. In this paper, we identify two levels of misalignment in existing A2V caching methods: temporal-semantic and computation-storage misalignment. To address them, we propose EchoCache, an energy-guided cross-modal caching framework for efficient A2V generation. EchoCache leverages audio time-frequency energy as a saliency anchor to guide latent-level cache updates and further introduces a dynamic timestep-latent caching mechanism with quantized cache management for joint efficiency and memory optimization. Extensive experiments on mainstream A2V models show that EchoCache consistently improves the latency-quality trade-off while preserving generation quality and audio-visual consistency. In particular, on Wan2.2-S2V over the EMTD benchmark, EchoCache achieves a 2.46x speedup with the best overall performance. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/EchoCache.

</details>


### [274] [Fermat Active Laplace Learning for Semi-Supervised Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02483)
*Vutichart Buranasiri,James M. Murphy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于HSI分类的主动学习算法，结合密度感知的Fermat距离与泊松加权调和标签传播（PWLL）。FALL利用Fermat距离构建亲和矩阵，而A-FALL通过地标点加速计算，实验验证了二者的有效性及A-FALL在大规模HSI场景中的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱图像分类需要大量标注数据，而标注成本高昂。现有主动学习算法在利用数据流形结构和密度信息方面存在不足，需要更有效的主动查询策略来提升标注效率。

Method: 提出FALL和A-FALL两种算法。FALL用Fermat距离构建全数据集亲和矩阵，并结合最小范数采集函数的对角扰动运行PWLL。A-FALL通过最远点采样选取地标像素，用地标多维缩放构建亲和矩阵以降低计算复杂度，并利用留一交叉验证自动选择Fermat指数p。

Result: 在Salinas A和Pavia数据集上进行实验，验证了FALL的有效性以及A-FALL在大规模高光谱图像场景中的可扩展性，两种方法均能提升标签精度。

Conclusion: Fermat距离与泊松加权调和标签传播的结合能够提供密度感知的数据流形估计，有效提升HSI分类的标注精度，且A-FALL通过地标采样策略在保证精度的同时实现了大规模数据的可扩展性。

Abstract: Two active learning algorithms for hyperspectral image (HSI) classification are proposed that combine density-aware Fermat distances with Poisson-reweighted harmonic label propagation. Our methods actively query points using an uncertainty-based acquisition function, extending Poisson ReWeighted Laplace Learning (PWLL). Our first algorithm, Fermat Active Laplace Learning (FALL), builds an affinity matrix using Fermat distances between all data points. Then, PWLL is run with a diagonal perturbation using the minimum-norm acquisition function. In contrast, Approximate FALL (A-FALL) computes Fermat distances between each data point and landmark pixels selected via farthest-point sampling and constructs the affinity matrix using landmark multidimensional scaling. After several query rounds, A-FALL selects the Fermat exponent $p$ using a leave-one-out cross-validation variant. FALL and A-FALL leverage Fermat distances and subsequent harmonic label propagation to provide a density-aware estimation of the data manifold, improving labeling accuracy. Experiments on Salinas A and Pavia show the effectiveness of FALL and the scalability of A-FALL to large HSI scenes.

</details>


### [275] [DyFrDet: Towards Accurate Small Object Detection via Dynamic Frequency Suppression with Label Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02495)
*Zihan Yang,Yang Guo,Hongxing Zhang,Dan Lu,Siyuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DyFrDet小目标检测器，通过动态抑制频域背景干扰和标签歧义来解决小目标定位难题。包含动态频率感知特征金字塔网络（DyFrFPN）和标签消歧模块（LDM），在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小目标检测因视觉线索不足而困难，以往工作多聚焦于构建判别性表征，但忽略了频域噪声和标签歧义问题，这两者严重阻碍了小目标的精确定位。

Method: 提出DyFrDet检测器：(1) DyFrFPN将层级特征变换到频域，通过动态频带预测器（DBP）自适应抑制低频冗余和高频噪声，保留小目标的判别性频域成分；(2) 标签消歧模块（LDM）利用概率分布显式建模并缓解目标标签的内在歧义，提升低分辨率小目标的定位精度。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到最先进性能，验证了该方法在各种挑战性场景下的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过同时处理频域噪声和标签歧义两个被忽视的问题，DyFrDet有效提升小目标检测的定位精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable progress over the past decades, accurately identifying small objects remains challenging because of their insufficient visual cues. Previous works typically attempt to construct discriminative representation of the small objects. However, the wide range frequency domain noises and label ambiguities have been greatly overlooked, which significantly hinders the accurate localization. To address these issues, we propose a novel small object detection (SOD) detector termed DyFrDet, which is able to precisely localize the small object by dynamically suppressing the background distractions in frequency domain. Specifically, we propose a Dynamic Frequency-aware Feature Pyramid Network (DyFrFPN) to adaptively suppress low-frequency redundancy and excessive high-frequency noises. The DyFrFPN transforms the hierarchical features into frequency domain representation, and introduces a Dynamic Band Predictor (DBP) to preserve the discriminative components for small object identification. Afterwards, we present a novel Label Disambiguation Module (LDM), which leverages probabilistic distributions to explicitly model and alleviate the inherent ambiguity of target labels, yielding efficient improvement in localization precision of the small objects with low-resolution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DyFrDet achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, indicating its effectiveness and robustness in various challenging scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ManOfStory/DyFrDet.

</details>


### [276] [Token Radius Attention for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02504)
*Jiayu Chen,Zhikun Jiang,Maoliang Li,Jiayi Luo,Jiawei Yang,Zihao Zheng,Hengyi Zhang,Guojie Luo,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Token Radius Attention (TRA)，一种训练无关的框架，利用查询熵与注意力密度之间的对数线性关系，将注意力预算映射为随时间衰减的半径，从而在视频扩散Transformer中实现稀疏注意力加速，仅保留9-19%的注意力交互即可获得1.56x-2.05x加速且保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer(VDiTs)虽能实现高保真生成，但密集3D自注意力带来二次方计算成本。现有头部级和块级稀疏方法对所有查询均匀分配计算预算，忽略了不同查询token的注意力需求差异，导致计算资源分配不均、效率低下的问题。

Method: TRA框架包括：(1) 基于观察——保留密度随查询变化且与注意力熵呈对数线性关系，以及主导注意力交互形成以查询为中心的、半径因token而异的邻域——将查询熵映射为解析式token预算，并转换为时间衰减半径，无需显式键排序；(2) 融合熵提取、预热重用和块稀疏掩码构建以进一步降低开销；(3) 整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在七个Wan2.1、Wan2.2和HunyuanVideo的T2V/I2V配置上，TRA仅保留9-19%的注意力交互，实现1.56x-2.05x加速，同时生成质量具有竞争力，代码已开源。

Conclusion: TRA验证了注意力需求因查询而异的核心假设，通过熵驱动的自适应稀疏策略在计算效率和生成质量之间取得了良好平衡，为视频扩散模型的注意力加速提供了一种高效且无需训练的新方案。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers (VDiTs) enable high-fidelity generation but incur quadratic cost from dense 3D self-attention. Existing head- and block-level sparse methods share computation budgets across queries, overlooking token-specific attention demand. We observe that retained density varies across queries yet correlates log-linearly with attention entropy, while dominant interactions form query-centered neighborhoods with token-dependent radii. Based on these findings, we propose Token Radius Attention (TRA), a training-free framework that maps query entropy to an analytic token budget and converts it into a temporally decayed radius without explicit key ranking. Fused entropy extraction, warm-up reuse, and block-sparse mask construction further reduce overhead. Across seven Wan2.1, Wan2.2, and HunyuanVideo T2V/I2V configurations, TRA retains only 9-19% of attention interactions and achieves 1.56x-2.05x speedup with competitive generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/IF-LAB-PKU/Token-Radius-Attention.

</details>


### [277] [ReMiX-MAE: Learning Missing-Channel Cross-Modal Representations from RGB-Only Clinical Facial Videos for Sympathetic-Mediated Pain Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02561)
*Nan Bi,Taoyue Wang,Lijun Yin,Vandana Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMiX-MAE，一种自监督多模态掩码预训练框架，利用同步RGB、热成像和深度视频学习可迁移的面部疼痛表示，并显式训练对缺失模态的鲁棒性，实现仅RGB部署下的高效疼痛评估。同时收集了SMP临床数据集，在多种评估设置下优于RGB-only基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床自动化疼痛评估面临两大挑战：临床真实面部视频数据稀缺且仅有弱标签（序列级自报告）；疼痛线索在RGB中可能微妙或接近中性，而热成像和深度信号虽信息丰富却难以在日常临床中部署。

Method: 提出ReMiX-MAE框架：(1) 自监督多模态掩码预训练，从同步RGB、热成像和深度视频学习可迁移面部表示；(2) 显式训练对缺失模态的鲁棒性，支持仅RGB部署；(3) 收集SMP数据集，包含跨多次就诊的纵向治疗前后配对录像；(4) 评估方式包括直接特征提取和从RGB解码的伪多模态特征。

Result: 在SMP数据集上，ReMiX-MAE在RGB-only部署下持续优于RGB-only掩码自编码器基线；在五分类挑战性设置中，伪多模态特征带来额外增益。在外部数据集上，ReMiX-MAE展现出更强的鲁棒性和标签效率迁移能力。

Conclusion: ReMiX-MAE通过多模态自监督预训练和缺失模态鲁棒性训练，成功弥合了丰富多模态信息与临床RGB-only部署之间的差距，在数据受限的临床环境中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Automated pain assessment in real clinics is limited by scarce clinically grounded facial video data with weak labels (often sequence-level self-report) and by the fact that pain cues can be subtle or near-neutral in RGB, while thermal and depth signals are informative yet impractical to deploy routinely. To address these challenges, we propose ReMiX-MAE (Reconstructing Missing Channel Cross-Modal Masked Autoencoder), a self-supervised multimodal masked pretraining framework that learns transferable facial representations from synchronized RGB, thermal, and depth videos and explicitly trains robustness to missing modalities, enabling RGB-only deployment. To fill the gap of clinically grounded facial pain data with video-level self-report and longitudinal treatment trajectories, we collect the Sympathetic Mediated Pain (SMP) dataset with paired pre- and post-recordings across multiple visits. Under RGB-only deployment, we evaluate ReMiX-MAE using both direct feature extraction and pseudo-multimodal features decoded from RGB. ReMiX-MAE consistently outperforms an RGB-only masked autoencoder baseline on SMP, with pseudo-multimodal features providing additional gains in the challenging five-class setting. Across external datasets, ReMiX-MAE further shows more robust and label-efficient transfer than RGB-only baselines, highlighting its advantage in data-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [278] [CAPEval: A Decoupled Caption Evaluation across Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02589)
*Zhipeng Liu,Haochen Wang,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPEval基准，将字幕质量解耦为Coverage（视觉信息覆盖度）和Precision（声明事实正确率）两个维度，并通过受控下游实验发现：Coverage与理解任务性能强相关，Precision与生成任务性能强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字幕质量评估将不同性质的质量属性混为一个标量目标，无法区分字幕覆盖了多少视觉信息与图像在多大程度上支持其声明的准确性，导致对下游任务缺乏精细诊断与可操作的指导。

Method: 构建CAPEval基准，包含人工撰写的真实标注字幕和人工验证的原子检查清单。将字幕质量分解为Coverage和Precision两个维度，选取10个字幕生成器，并在4个模型族中进行受控下游端到端实验，仅改变字幕来源这一变量。

Result: 发现一致的任务依赖性分离：Coverage是理解性能的更强相关因子，而Precision是生成性能的主导预测因子。

Conclusion: 该解耦评估范式不仅能提供更细粒度的字幕质量诊断，还能为不同下游任务定制字幕生成器的选择与优化提供可操作指导。

Abstract: Captions serve as a primary supervision signal for both multimodal understanding and text-to-image generation. However, previous evaluations treat the caption quality as a single scalar objective, which conflates two distinct properties: (1) how much visual information a caption covers and (2) how reliably the image supports its stated claims. To this end, we design a decoupled caption evaluation benchmark, CAPEval (Coverage And Precision Evaluation), with human-written ground-truth captions and human-verified atomic checklist items. Specifically, CAPEval decomposes caption quality into Coverage and Precision. The former quantifies how thoroughly a caption covers ground-truth factual content, while the latter reflects the factual correctness rate of all claims expressed in the caption. We select 10 captioners and further conduct controlled downstream end-to-end experiments with them from four model families, where the caption source is the only variable. Empirically, we find a consistent task-dependent dissociation: Coverage serves as the stronger correlate for understanding performance, whereas Precision acts as the dominant predictor for generation performance. This decoupled evaluation paradigm not only delivers a more fine-grained diagnosis of caption quality, but also offers actionable guidance for selecting and optimizing captioners tailored to different downstream tasks.

</details>


### [279] [VR3D: View-Robust 3D Representation Learning for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02598)
*Chao Ji,Shiyu Xuan,Zechao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VR3D框架，通过将2D图像特征提升到统一的3D规范空间来解耦视角偏差，解决航拍-地面行人重识别中的跨平台视角变化问题，在三个基准数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅在2D图像空间学习视角不变表征，剧烈的视角变化导致所学特征仍与视角偏差耦合，无法有效解决航拍-地面行人重识别中严重的遮挡和几何形变问题。

Method: 提出VR3D框架，包含两个核心模块：1) View-Robust 3D Representation Interaction (VR3I)，利用从2D观测提取的3D先验将2D外观特征提升到规范3D空间，并通过3D几何-语义注意力建立2D补丁与3D体素之间的交互；2) Reliability-Aware Fusion，根据样本不同的3D重建可靠性自适应聚合多源表征。

Result: 在CARGO、AG-ReID.v1和AG-ReID.v2三个基准数据集上取得了优于现有方法的结果，其中在CARGO上Rank-1指标提升5.63%。

Conclusion: 通过将特征表示从2D图像空间提升到统一的3D规范空间并结合可靠性感知融合，VR3D有效解耦了视角偏差，验证了3D表征学习在跨视角行人重识别中的有效性。

Abstract: Aerial-ground person re-identification is a challenging task due to cross-platform viewpoint variations, which cause severe occlusion and geometric deformation. Existing methods attempt to learn view-invariant representations exclusively within the 2D image space, where drastic viewpoint variations cause the learned features to remain coupled with viewpoint bias. To address this, we propose VR3D, a View-Robust 3D Representation Learning framework that maps images into a unified 3D coordinate space to achieve view-independent feature interaction. Specifically, we introduce View-Robust 3D Representation Interaction, which leverages 3D priors extracted from single 2D observations to lift 2D appearance features into a canonical 3D space. VR3I employs 3D Geometry-Semantic Attention to establish interactions between 2D patches and 3D voxels from corresponding body parts based on their 3D spatial locations, effectively grounding 2D semantics within a 3D framework. In addition, as the reliability of these representations varies across samples due to viewpoint changes and 3D reconstruction errors, we introduce Reliability-Aware Fusion, which estimates sample-specific reliability and adaptively aggregates the multi-source representations. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets (CARGO, AG-ReID.v1, and AG-ReID.v2) demonstrate that VR3D outperforms recent methods. For example, it achieves a 5.63% improvement in Rank-1 on CARGO. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [280] [WorldExam: Benchmarking World Models from Apparent Appearance to Inherent Reactivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02603)
*Yuxue Yang,Shuyao Shang,Jiahe Wang,Zitong Zhou,Liang Tan,Junhan Zeng,Ruizhi Li,Junyan Li,Yu Liu,Xiao Yang,Yong Li,Jun Zhu,Hongsheng Li,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WorldExam，一个分层的视频生成模型诊断基准，涵盖视觉质量、控制遵从、空间一致性和世界反应性四个层级，共1,474个案例、八个任务。通过对20个代表性模型的评估，揭示了不同范式（相机驱动、动作驱动、语言驱动）模型的能力分化，且没有任何模型能同时兼顾广泛任务覆盖和一致的高表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控视频生成模型正日益被视为世界模型，但其评估不应仅停留在视觉质量和显式指令完成度上，还应考察世界的内在反应性——即模型能否从场景状态推断世界的合理反应并生成未明确指定的后果。现有基准主要评估视觉质量或显式指令执行，忽略了这一关键维度。

Method: 构建了WorldExam分层诊断基准，包含四个层级（视觉质量、控制遵从、空间一致性、世界反应性）和八个专项任务，共1,474个测试案例。基准支持对相机驱动、动作驱动和语言驱动三类模型范式的统一评估，其中世界反应性层级专门评估场景条件反应和目标导向行为。

Result: 对20个代表性模型的评估显示：相机驱动模型在相机控制上表现出色但界面不支持动态交互；动作驱动模型能更精确地控制主体但世界往往缺乏响应；语言驱动模型在交互方面表现更好但对复杂控制的遵从度较低。没有任何模型能兼顾广泛任务覆盖和一致的高性能表现。

Conclusion: 高视觉质量和显式指令完成度并不能保证世界的内在反应性，现有视频生成模型在世界反应性方面仍有显著不足，需要新的评估方法和模型设计来弥合这一差距。

Abstract: Controllable video generation models are increasingly being developed as world models. Accordingly, evaluating them in this role extends beyond the apparent appearance of generated videos to the inherent reactivity of the worlds they depict: the ability to infer from the scene state how the world should react and to generate plausible consequences not explicitly described in the input. Yet existing benchmarks mainly assess visual quality or explicit instruction fulfillment by checking whether requested actions and interaction outcomes are realized, leaving inherent reactivity underexamined. We introduce WorldExam, a hierarchical diagnostic benchmark spanning four levels: Visual Quality, Control Adherence, Spatial Consistency, and World Reactivity. It comprises 1,474 cases across eight dedicated tasks and supports unified evaluation of camera-, action-, and language-driven model paradigms. The World Reactivity level evaluates scene-conditioned reactions and goal-directed behaviors beyond what is explicitly specified in the input. Evaluation of 20 representative models reveals a clear capability split. Camera-driven models excel at camera control, but their interfaces do not support dynamic interaction; action-driven models control subjects more precisely but often leave the world unresponsive; and language-driven models perform better on interaction but follow complex controls less faithfully. No model combines broad task coverage with consistently strong performance, showing that high visual quality and explicit instruction fulfillment do not guarantee inherent reactivity.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [281] [Cost-Effective Automated Judging of Natural-Language Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00004)
*Benjamin Grayzel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了廉价开源权重模型能否作为自然语言数学证明的可靠评判者。在IMO-GradingBench上，三个廉价模型（GPT-OSS 120B、DeepSeek-V4 Flash、Gemma-4 31B）在人类通过/失败判定上与Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro等前沿模型统计上无显著差异，但成本降低了最多100倍。推荐三人一致通过（all-three-pass）规则作为部署默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估数学推理系统中的自然语言证明需要频繁的人工评分，而前沿大模型评判者成本高昂。作者希望探索是否可以用廉价开源权重模型替代前沿模型作为可靠的证明评判者，以降低成本。

Method: 在IMO-GradingBench的200实例验证样本上，比较三个廉价评判模型（GPT-OSS 120B、DeepSeek-V4 Flash、Gemma-4 31B）与前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini 3.1 Pro）在给定候选证明、真实证明和人工评分标准下对人类通过/失败判定的认同率。随后扩展到完整的1000实例基准，探索包括多数投票和全体一致在内的共识规则。

Result: 三个廉价评判模型与人类判定的一致率在统计上与前沿模型无显著差异，成本降低1-2个数量级。多数投票与最强成员表现相当但未提升；要求全体一致通过（all-three-pass）能达到最高的通过一致率和精确率，且在四次重复试验中波动最小。

Conclusion: 廉价开源权重模型可以以1-2个数量级的成本优势达到与前沿模型相当的评判性能。all-three-pass规则被推荐为部署默认方案，但需注意该规则是事后识别的，需要独立复制验证。

Abstract: Grading natural-language mathematical proofs is a recurring cost in evaluating math-reasoning systems, and frontier LLM judges are expensive. We ask whether cheap open-weight models can serve as reliable judges given a candidate proof, a ground-truth proof, and a human-grading rubric. On a 200-instance validation sample of IMO-GradingBench, three cheap judges (GPT-OSS 120B, DeepSeek-V4 Flash, Gemma-4 31B) agree with human pass/fail decisions at rates statistically indistinguishable from Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro, at up to $100\times$ lower cost. We had expected a majority vote of the three to be the best budget option; it matched the frontier but did not improve on its strongest member. Extending to the full 1000-instance benchmark and exploring consensus rules, we found that requiring unanimous agreement (all-three-pass) reaches the highest pass-agreement and precision and, on four replicate runs, the smallest run-to-run spread. The headline finding is that cheap judges are competitive with the frontier at one to two orders of magnitude lower cost; as a deployable default we recommend all-three-pass, with the caveat that this rule was identified post-hoc and warrants independent replication.

</details>


### [282] [RubricReviewer: From Direct Critique to Objective and Comprehensive Rubric-Driven Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00005)
*Shuyu Guo,Wenxiang Hu,Yuyue Zhao,Yougang Lyu,Xiaohui Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RubricReviewer是一个完全基于rubric驱动的论文审稿框架，通过显式的rubric生成步骤，结合训练-free的Scout智能体（收集外部证据）和与人类对齐的Aligner模型（消费证据），同时解决现有LLM审稿系统在rubric隐式化和证据收集/判别判断分离上的两大结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流会议的同行评审面临空前的投稿压力，促使使用LLM作为审稿助手。但现有LLM审稿系统存在两个结构性缺陷：一是将论文直接映射到评审意见，使底层rubric隐式化，将rubric推导与判断纠缠在一起；二是现有范式各只捕捉了好审稿的一半能力——训练free的智能体能收集广泛证据但产生无方向性的批评，而基于训练的模型继承了人类判别性判断及其噪声和不均衡覆盖。

Method: RubricReviewer将rubric生成作为显式的中间步骤，使评审生成和最终评估都基于论文自适应的rubric。框架结合了训练free的智能体（Scout）负责收集外部证据，以及人类对齐的已训练模型（Aligner）负责消费这些证据，融合两种监督来源的优势。

Result: 在真实世界投稿上的实验表明，RubricReviewer生成的评审比现有系统明显更全面、更具判别力，并且在对抗提示注入攻击方面展现出最强的鲁棒性。消融研究进一步确认了每个组件的必要性。

Conclusion: RubricReviewer通过显式rubric生成和双组件融合架构，成功解决了LLM审稿系统中的结构性局限，实现了更全面、更判别性且更安全的自动论文评审。

Abstract: Peer review at major venues is under unprecedented submission pressure, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) as review assistants. Existing LLM-based reviewers, however, face two structural limitations. First, they map manuscripts directly to reviews, leaving the underlying rubric implicit and entangling its derivation with the judgement. Second, the prevailing paradigms each capture only half of a good review: training-free agents gather broad evidence but produce undirected critiques, while training-based reviewers inherit human discriminative judgement together with its noise and uneven coverage. We introduce RubricReviewer, a fully rubric-driven framework that addresses both limitations. It makes rubric generation an explicit intermediate step, so that both review generation and the final assessment are conditioned on paper-adaptive rubrics. It further combines a training-free agent (Scout) that gathers external evidence with a human-aligned trained model (Aligner) that consumes this evidence, fusing the strengths of both supervision sources. Experiments on real-world submissions show that RubricReviewer produces reviews that are markedly more comprehensive and more discriminative than prior systems, and exhibits the strongest robustness against adversarial prompt-injection attacks. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of each component.

</details>


### [283] [MemoryForge: Synthesize Lifelong Memory for Human-Like LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00007)
*Bohan Tang,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MemoryForge框架，通过基于记忆的条件化范式替代传统的基于提示的角色描述方法，为冻结的LLM合成自传体记忆库，使其能够动态检索情境相关记忆来展现更人性化的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于提示的方法通过注入静态文本档案来赋予LLM人格，但由于缺乏真实的生活记忆，往往导致智能体表现出泛化行为，难以实现逼真的人格化。

Method: MemoryForge包含三个核心组件：上下文生成器用于社会历史背景构建、生活组织器用于目标身份的连贯发展、多分辨率模拟器在广泛时间摘要与高保真情景体验之间取得平衡，实现从简要目标人格合成终身记忆。

Result: 在PersonaGym角色扮演和SimulatorArena用户模拟实验中，MemoryForge合成的记忆库使冻结LLM在多个指标和LLM骨干网络上均优于强描述性条件化基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于记忆的条件化范式相比传统提示方法更有效地为LLM赋予人性化人格，为角色扮演和用户模拟等智能体应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) with human-like personas is crucial for agentic applications, such as role-play and user simulation. Traditional prompt-based methods rely on descriptive conditioning by injecting static textual profiles, which often makes agents show generic behaviors due to a lack of realistic life memory. To fill this gap, we introduce memory-based conditioning, a paradigm inspired by the cognitive psychology, which replaces abstract profiles with an autobiographical memory base, enabling frozen LLMs to dynamically retrieve situation-relevant memory to guide their behaviors. We formalize its enabling task as customized lifelong memory synthesis and propose MemoryForge, a novel framework to synthesize such lifelong memory from brief target personas. MemoryForge has three key components: a context generator for socio-historical grounding, a life organizer for developmental coherence toward the target identity, and a multi-resolution simulator that balances broad temporal summaries with high-fidelity episodic experiences. Experiments on PersonaGym for role-play and SimulatorArena for user-simulation, show that the synthesized memory base by MemoryForge enables frozen LLMs to exhibit more human-like behaviors than strong descriptive conditioning baselines across multiple metrics and LLM backbones.

</details>


### [284] [AgentMemBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Memory Management Strategies in Conversational AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00009)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentMemBench，一个统一的基准框架，在相同条件下评估五种长期对话记忆管理策略（ICW、EKV、GEM、CBS、WAM），覆盖三个公开数据集和491个标注问答。结果显示EKV在所有质量指标上占优，尤其在长距离回忆上优势显著（LoCoMo上Recall@5达0.573，其他方法几乎为零），但代价是更高的内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI代理的有限上下文窗口无法支持跨数千轮的连贯回忆，长期记忆是当前的关键瓶颈。缺乏在相同条件下对不同记忆管理策略进行统一、可复现比较的基准。

Method: 构建AgentMemBench统一基准，在相同条件下评估五种记忆策略：上下文窗口（ICW）、外部键值存储（EKV）、图式情景记忆（GEM）、压缩摘要（CBS）和网络增强记忆（WAM）。使用三个数据集（LoCoMo、MultiDoc2Dial、MSC），以Recall@k、MRR、nDCG@k、Answer F1、LLM-judge Faithfulness、Memory Footprint和Latency为指标，生成和评判均使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（4-bit）贪心解码。

Result: EKV在所有质量维度上主导（宏Recall@5 0.792、MRR 0.677、F1 0.156、Faithfulness 0.354）；在LoCoMo长距离回忆任务中，ICW、WAM、GEM、CBS几乎无法检索（Recall@5≤0.005），而EKV达到0.573；CBS在检索上位居第二（0.556）；WAM在语料内回忆上与ICW持平；EKV的检索优势伴随约5,100 token vs 300 token的内存占用代价。

Conclusion: 密集检索（EKV）是唯一能在长距离记忆任务中有效扩展的方案，而近因窗口、摘要和知识图谱在长时程上失效。记忆管理存在明确的准确率-效率权衡，即EKV的高召回以更高内存占用为代价。此外，两篇已发表的记忆系统（MemGPT、HippoRAG）也在同一框架下被评估，所有代码和结果工件均已开源。

Abstract: Long-term memory remains a critical bottleneck for conversational AI agents, whose finite context windows cannot support coherent recall across thousands of turns. We present AgentMemBench, a unified, reproducible benchmark evaluating five memory management strategies under identical conditions: in-context windowing (ICW), external key-value store (EKV), graph-based episodic memory (GEM), compression-based summarisation (CBS), and web-augmented memory (WAM). All are assessed across three public datasets covering long-term multi-session dialogue (LoCoMo), task-oriented document grounding (MultiDoc2Dial), and persona-grounded multi-session chat (MSC), using Recall@k, MRR, nDCG@k, Answer F1, an LLM-judge Faithfulness score, Memory Footprint, and Latency over 491 annotated question turns. Generation and judging both use Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (4-bit), with greedy decoding for determinism. Our results show that (1) EKV dominates on every quality axis (macro Recall@5 0.792, MRR 0.677, F1 0.156, Faithfulness 0.354); (2) long-range recall is decisive: on LoCoMo, where the gold turn lies many sessions back, ICW, WAM, GEM, and CBS retrieve almost nothing (Recall@5 <= 0.005) while EKV alone reaches 0.573, showing that recency windows, summaries, and entity graphs collapse at long horizons and only dense retrieval scales; (3) CBS is the runner-up on retrieval (0.556); (4) WAM equals ICW on in-corpus recall by construction, since external results carry no in-corpus provenance; and (5) EKV's recall advantage carries a footprint cost (~5,100 vs ~300 tokens for ICW/WAM), an explicit accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We additionally evaluate two published memory systems (MemGPT/Letta, HippoRAG) against the same harness, and release all code, environment, and result artefacts for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [285] [DLLM-TTS: Block Discrete Diffusion Language Model for Text-to-Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00011)
*Wasim Madha,Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了DLLM-TTS框架，将文本转语音任务建模为基于X-Codec2音频编解码标记的块状离散扩散过程，在块内并行生成、跨块顺序处理，实现0.15的实时因子，仅用0.6B参数和20K小时数据即可达到SOTA级别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS系统面临两难：自回归编解码语言模型生成语音质量高但需要大规模模型和训练数据且逐token串行解码速度慢；非自回归方法虽快但语言准确性不足。

Method: 将TTS形式化为对X-Codec2神经音频编解码token的条件块状离散扩散过程。将序列分解为块，在块内应用掩码扩散，同时跨块顺序处理，同时学习局部声学连贯性和全局文本-语音对齐。推理时块内并行token预测实现高效生成。

Result: 在Seed-TTS-eval基准上，0.6B参数模型用20K小时训练数据取得有竞争力的性能，推理RTF为0.15，验证了块状离散扩散语言模型可实现实用且数据高效的并行语音合成。

Conclusion: 块状离散扩散语言模型在生成质量和推理效率之间取得了良好平衡，同时显著降低了模型规模和训练数据需求，为数据高效的并行TTS提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Current text-to-speech systems face a trade-off: autoregres- sive codec language models produce highly intelligible speech but require large-scale models and training data and decode tokens sequentially, while non-autoregressive approaches im- prove speed at the cost of linguistic accuracy. We present DLLM-TTS, a framework that formulates TTS as conditional block discrete diffusion over X-Codec2 neural audio codec to- kens. The model decomposes sequences into blocks and applies masked diffusion within each block while processing blocks se- quentially, learning both local acoustic coherence and global text-speech alignment. During inference, parallel token pre- diction within blocks enables efficient generation with a real- time factor (RTF) of 0.15. A 0.6B-parameter model trained on 20K hours achieves competitive performance on the Seed- TTS-eval benchmark, demonstrating that block discrete diffu- sion language models enable practical and data-efficient speech synthesis with parallel generation.

</details>


### [286] [Obshazard-bench: Benchmarking Multimodal Foundation Models for Real-Time Disaster Intelligence from Raw Earth Observation Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00012)
*Fengxiang Wang,Qiuyang Yu,Yueying Li,Mingshuo Chen,Chengchi Fei,Kaiyi Xu,Lixin Gu,Wangxu Wei,Junchao Gong,Lipeng Ma,Jiong Wang,Fenghua Ling,Wenlong Zhang,Xue Yang,Wenjing Yang,Ben Fei,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Obshazard-bench，一个面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的实时观测驱动灾害智能评测基准，直接整合高频卫星探测流、地面站观测、历史灾害记录和社会经济指标，覆盖8大灾害类别和28个子类、60多个国家、120多个极端事件案例及数千个VQA样本，并定义了三阶段评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基准大多依赖静态、事后且经专家处理的产品（如网格化再分析数据），难以与快速演变的实际灾害应急场景对齐。MLLMs在真实灾害应急响应中的能力尚未得到充分评测，需要一个新的评测基准来弥补这一空白。

Method: Obshazard-bench直接整合来自多种卫星的高频原始探测流与地面同步观测、历史灾害记录和社会经济指标，绕过延迟的专家处理和物理反演流程。涵盖8大灾害类别和28个子类、60多个国家、120多个历史极端事件案例及数千个生命周期导向的VQA样本，并定义了三阶段评测分类体系（预测性危机预警、活跃演化推理、多层面影响量化）。

Result: 在代表性通用型和地球聚焦型基础模型上的实验表明，这些模型在将原始多通道物理观测转化为具有时间基础和决策相关性的灾害推理方面存在显著局限。

Conclusion: Obshazard-bench填补了MLLMs在真实灾害应急智能评测方面的空白，揭示了现有基础模型在实时灾害推理能力上的不足，为未来模型改进提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret Earth observation data, yet their capability to support real-world disaster emergency response remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing remote sensing benchmarks largely rely on static, post-hoc, and expert-processed products, such as gridded reanalysis data, which are difficult to align with operational disaster scenarios where hazards evolve rapidly and decisions must be made under strict time constraints. To bridge this gap, we introduce Obshazard-bench, a real-time, observation-driven benchmark for evaluating disaster intelligence in MLLMs. Unlike image-centric or post-event benchmarks, Obshazard-bench directly integrates raw, high-frequency satellite sounding streams from diverse satellite sensors with concurrent ground-station observations, historical disaster records, and socio-economic indicators, bypassing delayed expert-processing and physical-inversion pipelines. The benchmark covers 8 major disaster categories and 28 sub-categories across more than 60 countries, incorporating over 120 historically documented extreme-event cases and thousands of lifecycle-oriented VQA samples. Moreover, Obshazard-bench further defines a three-stage evaluation taxonomy aligned with the operational disaster workflow: Predictive Crisis Anticipation for pre-disaster risk detection and early forecasting, Active Evolution Reasoning for in-situ disaster tracking and termination prediction, and Multi-faceted Impact Quantification for post-disaster magnitude deduction, humanitarian burden estimation, and socio-economic impact assessment. Experiments on representative general-purpose and Earth-focused foundation models reveal substantial limitations in transforming raw multi-channel physical observations into temporally grounded and decision-relevant disaster reasoning.

</details>


### [287] [What Transfers from Text to Vision? Capability Scaling Laws and Transfer Dynamics for VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00013)
*Ziran Li,Qiang Wang,Zhengyu Chen,Shanglin Lei,Borun Chen,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出能力驱动的多模态缩放定律（CDM Scaling Law），这是首个跨模型家族框架，能从LLM文本基准测试中直接提取能力分数来预测VLM性能，无需预先训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择LLM主干是构建VLM时最重要的决策，但现有方法缺乏原则性指导：基于计算量的缩放定律无法跨模型家族泛化，也没有框架能在训练前直接预测VLM性能。

Method: 通过PCA从LLM文本基准测试中提取低维能力分数S，将VLM性能建模为S的函数，包含每主干迁移率和吸收率两个参数。在7个模型家族、34个LLM上训练了150多个VLM。

Result: 该定律能够将迁移率从8B参数量外推到72B规模的主干，高保真预测完整VLM训练轨迹，并泛化到完全未见的模型家族。同时发现：某些文本基准与多模态性能负相关、基础LLM优于指令微调版本作为VLM主干、不同模型家族在迁移-吸收空间中占据不同位置。

Conclusion: 该框架将主干选择从昂贵的经验测试转变为有原则的定量决策，提供了跨家族可泛化的缩放定律和可操作的洞察。

Abstract: Choosing the right large language model (LLM) backbone is the most consequential decision when building a vision-language model (VLM), yet it remains fundamentally unprincipled: compute-based scaling laws fail to generalize across model families, and no framework exists for directly predicting VLM performance before training begins. We propose the Capability-Driven Multimodal Scaling Law, the first cross-family framework that predicts VLM benchmark accuracy from directly observable textual capability. Given a low-dimensional capability score $S$ extracted from LLM textual benchmarks via PCA, we model VLM performance as a function of $S$, with a per-backbone transfer rate and an absorption rate that quantifies data-scaling efficiency. To fit and validate the framework, we train over 150 VLMs on 34 LLMs spanning 7 model families under a strictly controlled recipe. Evaluations on more than 200 textual and 50 multimodal benchmarks show that the law accurately extrapolates transfer rate from models up to 8B parameters to 72B-scale backbones, predicts full VLM training trajectories with high fidelity, and generalizes to entirely held-out model families. Beyond the scaling law, our analysis surfaces actionable insights: certain textual benchmarks negatively correlate with multimodal performance, exposing latent benchmark-gaming behavior; base LLMs outperform instruction-tuned counterparts as VLM backbones due to higher absorption rates and lower data-scaling decay; and different model families occupy distinct positions in the transfer--absorption space. The framework turns backbone selection from costly empirical sweeps into a principled, quantitative decision. Code and data are available at https://github.com/wangq-dev/CDMScaling.

</details>


### [288] [Role Steering of Language Models for Social Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00023)
*Isaac Song,Mohammed Rehan Parwani,Glenn Matlin,Emile Anand,Akhil Theerthala,Arjun Chatterjee,Maria Kostylew,Yonadav G. Shavit,Sebastien Krier,Mark Riedl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活引导（activation-steering）筛选的工作流，用于角色条件化的语言模型代理。通过定义角色档案、提取角色特定方向、扫描四种转向系数、评估角色档案对齐度，对每个候选配置进行通过或标记。在OLMo-3-7B-Instruct上对275个角色、228个角色无关问题进行了验证，角色特定方向比对照组的对齐评分更高（63.2 vs 41.1），且保留更高词汇多样性。核心实践输出是角色级筛选：大多数角色随转向增强而改善，但38个角色在所有六个维度上均下降，表明应逐角色选择系数而非统一高强度部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于语言模型代理构建的社交模拟需要角色条件化行为，并且该行为需在代理放入模拟群体之前得到验证。现有方法缺乏系统性的角色适配合格的筛选流程，因此需要一种可预先检查角色条件化行为的工作流。

Method: 提出激活引导筛选工作流：(1) 定义角色档案；(2) 提取角色特定方向；(3) 扫描四种转向系数；(4) 评估角色档案对齐度；(5) 对每个候选配置通过或标记。在OLMo-3-7B-Instruct上使用275个角色、228个角色无关问题、GPT-4.1-mini提示的角色参考和GPT-4.1-mini评审者进行评估。

Result: 角色特定方向比先前人格向量研究中的助手轴方向对照获得更高的评审角色档案对齐度（平均总分63.2 vs 41.1）。角色特定方向保留高词汇多样性，而对照组在较大系数时词汇多样性急剧下降。筛选显示大多数角色随转向增强而改善，但38个角色在所有六个维度上均下降。

Conclusion: 角色级筛选是主要实用输出：由于部分角色在转向增强时产生退化，模拟构建者应逐角色选择转向系数，而非采用统一的高强度设置。工作流为角色条件化代理提供了一种可预先验证、系统化的筛选方法。

Abstract: Social simulations built from language-model agents need role-conditioned behavior that can be checked before agents are placed into a simulated population. We introduce an activation-steering screening workflow for role-conditioned agents: define a role profile, extract a role-specific direction, sweep four steering coefficients, evaluate role-profile alignment, and pass or flag each candidate configuration. On OLMo-3-7B-Instruct, we apply the workflow to a mixed 275-role inventory with 228 role-agnostic questions, GPT-4.1-mini prompted role references, and GPT-4.1-mini judges. Role-specific directions receive higher judged role-profile alignment than an assistant-axis directional control from prior persona-vector work, with mean overall scores of 63.2 versus 41.1 across the tested grid. They also preserve high lexical diversity, while the control drops sharply at larger coefficients. The role-level screen is the main practical output: most roles improve as steering increases, but 38 roles decline across all six measured dimensions, showing why simulation builders should choose coefficients per role rather than deploy a uniform high-strength setting. We make our code and evaluation artifacts available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/anonymous-research-code-5F03/.

</details>


### [289] [Exploring More to Solve More: Boosting Diversity in Text Diffusion Models via Entropy-Based Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00024)
*Jingwei Zhang,Haoyu Lei,Zijin Feng,Jiacheng Sun,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为SAKE（语义感知核熵）的训练-free引导方法，通过计算核Gram矩阵上的二阶Rényi熵捕捉跨词元语义交互和相对位置信息，在线性化后动态调整采样分布，以在保真度和多样性之间取得更优的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在连续域（如图像生成）取得了巨大成功，但将其可控性推广到离散、序列化的文本生成仍面临挑战。现有采样策略和引导方法仅调整词元似然，未能捕捉更广泛的语义景观，导致保真度与多样性之间的次优权衡。

Method: 提出SAKE引导方法：(1) 计算阶数为2的Rényi熵（基于核Gram矩阵），该矩阵同时编码跨词元语义交互和相对词元位置信息；(2) 在嵌入空间中对目标函数进行线性化，推导出易于处理的引导信号；(3) 该信号动态调整采样分布——在冗余时展平分布以鼓励探索，在多样化时锐化分布以提升保真度。

Result: 实验表明，SAKE方法在保真度与多样性之间实现了更优的Pareto前沿，并在推理密集型任务（如代码生成和数学生成）的多采样性能上优于温度缩放和离散引导基线方法。

Conclusion: SAKE作为一种无需训练、计算可行的文本生成引导方法，能够通过语义感知的熵度量动态平衡采样分布，有效提升了文本生成在推理任务中的整体质量。

Abstract: Although diffusion models have revolutionized continuous domains like image synthesis through high quality generations and controllable guidance mechanisms, bringing this controllability to the discrete, sequential nature of text remains an open challenge. Meanwhile, current sampling strategies and guidance methods adjust token likelihoods without capturing the broader semantic landscape, leading to a suboptimal balance between fidelity and diversity. In this work, we introduce a novel training-free Semantic-Aware Kernel Entropy (SAKE) guidance method. Our method computes the order-2 Rényi entropy over a kernel Gram matrix that captures both cross-token semantic interactions and relative token positions. By linearizing this objective in the embedding space, we derive a tractable guidance signal that dynamically adjusts the sampling distribution, flattening it to encourage exploration during redundancy and sharpening it for fidelity when diverse. Empirical experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves a superior Pareto frontier between fidelity and diversity, and improves multi-sample performance on reasoning-intensive tasks, such as code and mathematics generation, compared to temperature scaling and discrete guidance baselines.

</details>


### [290] [SLMs as Multi-Agent Routers: A Progressive SFT and Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00030)
*Gayathri V Kondapalli,Alexander Ng,Hirsh Pithadia,Rahul Monish,Harvey Yorke,Amir Kayhani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过监督微调结合强化学习训练的小语言模型，用于联合执行检索智能体选择和下游工具调用的结构化参数生成。该方法利用基于检索相关性和查询-智能体主题对齐的分层奖励函数，让模型从检索性能中学习任务相关的智能体适用性，解决了传统基于意图的路由方法无法感知检索内容质量的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的查询路由方法基于推断的主题或意图来选择检索智能体，但意图驱动的选择存在根本性局限：缺乏来自检索内容的信号，且无法检测到主题匹配但检索结果低相关性的情况。

Method: 训练一个小语言模型，先进行监督微调，再通过强化学习优化。模型同时执行智能体选择和下游工具调用的结构化参数生成，使用分层奖励函数，该函数结合了检索相关性指标和查询-智能体主题对齐程度。

Result: 在智能体-查询不匹配的目标子集上，模型NDCG@10达到0.918，远优于两个仅基于意图路由的LLM基线（亚马逊Nova Lite的0.539和Claude Haiku 4.5的0.490）。整体平均NDCG@10为0.771，比Nova Lite高0.177，比Haiku高0.219。平均选择延迟为120.1ms，比Nova Lite减少82.4%。

Conclusion: 通过让模型从实际检索性能中学习智能体适用性，而非仅依赖表面主题匹配，可以显著提升查询路由质量，同时大幅降低延迟，证明基于检索反馈的学习比纯意图推理更有效。

Abstract: Specialised retrieval agents typically surface higher quality results than general-purpose search, but selecting the optimal agent for a given query remains an open problem. Current approaches route queries based on inferred topic or intent, however intent-based selection is fundamentally limited: it does not incorporate signal from retrieved content, and cannot detect when a topically aligned agent produces low-relevance results. We address this by training a small language model via supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning to jointly perform agent selection and structured parameter generation for downstream tool calls, using a hierarchical reward function grounded in retrieval relevance along with query-agent topic alignment. This enables the model to learn task-dependent agent suitability from retrieval performance: which agents reliably yield high-relevance results for which query distributions, and when to redirect queries away from specialised agents despite surface-level topical overlap. On a targeted subset of such agent-query mismatches, the trained model achieves an NDCG@10 of 0.918 compared to 0.539 and 0.490 for two LLM baselines (Amazon Nova Lite and Claude Haiku 4.5) that route on intent alone. Overall, it achieves a mean NDCG@10 of 0.771 (+0.177 over Nova Lite, +0.219 over Haiku) with a mean selection latency of 120.1ms, an 82.4% reduction over Nova Lite.

</details>


### [291] [XL-DocBench: Benchmarking Evidence-Grounded Extra-Long Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00036)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Ruichun Ma,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍XL-DocBench，一个面向超长文档理解的基准测试，包含1,519个人工验证的问题，覆盖6个专业领域，上下文最长可达2,303页。基准包含多证据页、图表推理、跨文档问题及12种推理标签和类型化验证规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中专业任务（合规、临床、金融、工程）需要LLM理解数百乃至数千页的长文档，且决策必须可追溯到具体的证据页。然而现有基准大多只评测短上下文或单页问答，缺乏对超长文档理解能力的全面评测。

Method: 采用树状引导的合成管线构建基准，配合人工过滤和194名人类专家的全面验证。问题具有12种推理标签、专家标注的证据页、类型化验证规则和答案格式，包含218个无答案案例。超过72%的问题需要多证据页，36.6%涉及表格/图表/图形，10.9%需要跨文档证据。

Result: 实验表明当前系统在长上下文、多页证据和结构化专业文档推理方面仍然表现不佳。该基准允许将系统失败归因于检索、证据使用或规则遵循等具体环节，而非单一的排行榜分数。

Conclusion: XL-DocBench填补了先前单页、短多页或纯文本长上下文基准的空白，通过将超长专业上下文与页面级证据和类型化规则相结合，为未来长文档理解研究提供了更全面的评测框架。

Abstract: Real-world document tasks often ask professionals to answer questions from annual reports, regulations, clinical guidelines, and technical manuals that span hundreds or thousands of pages. Some questions also require comparing related reports. Reliable long-document understanding is therefore a prerequisite for using LLMs in compliance, clinical, financial, and engineering workflows, where decisions must be traceable to specific evidence pages and the cost of an unsupported answer is high -- yet most existing benchmarks still measure short-context or single-page QA. We introduce XL-DocBench, a fully human-verified benchmark for extra-long document understanding, with 1,519 retained questions from six professional domains and contexts up to 2,303 pages. XL-DocBench goes beyond page-level lookup. 1,103 examples (72.6\%) use multiple evidence pages. The final set also includes 556 questions (36.6\%) that use tables, charts, or figures, and 165 questions (10.9\%) that require evidence from multiple documents. Each question has one of twelve reasoning labels, expert-annotated evidence pages, a typed verification rule, and an answer format, including 218 None-answer cases. We build the benchmark with a tree-guided synthesis pipeline followed by artifact filters and full verification by 194 human experts. By coupling extra-long professional contexts with page-level evidence and typed rules, XL-DocBench fills a gap left by prior single-page, short multi-page, or text-only long-context benchmarks, and lets future work attribute system failures to retrieval, evidence use, or rule following rather than to a single leaderboard score. The results show that current systems still struggle with long contexts, multi-page evidence, and structured reasoning over professional documents.

</details>


### [292] [Trustworthiness Costs of Domain Adaptation in Small Language Models:A Cross-Architecture Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00042)
*Ramesh B. Paramkusham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地量化了小语言模型（SLMs）领域适应过程中的可信度代价，涵盖3种架构、3个领域、2种训练数据条件和4种微调策略共216种实验配置。研究发现：基线QLoRA领域适应对事实校准影响极小；对抗扰动数据在不损害可信度的前提下提升适应质量；三种安全保持策略均未能降低对抗危害易感性，其中Dark ER和TA-LoRA反而显著增加了有害响应率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调在SLM领域适应中的性能收益已有充分研究，但其对模型可信度（事实校准和对抗鲁棒性）的影响尚不明确。在医疗、法律、金融等高风险场景中部署领域适应SLM，需要量化此类适应策略对模型可信度的影响。

Method: 在216种实验配置下（3种架构、3个领域、2种数据条件、4种微调策略、3个随机种子），使用TruthfulQA MC2评估事实校准，HarmBench ASR评估对抗鲁棒性。微调策略包括基线LoRA、Safety-DPO、Dark Experience Replay和Task Arithmetic LoRA。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 基线QLoRA领域适应对TruthfulQA MC2影响极小（平均变化<0.02）；(2) 对抗扰动数据持续改善领域适应质量（loss下降约0.040）且不损害可信度基准；(3) Safety-DPO基本中性（ASR变化<0.001），但Dark ER和TA-LoRA在安全对齐模型中使平均HarmBench ASR分别增加+0.171和+0.155，个别配置超过+0.45。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，回放类和算术合并类策略并不能将安全对齐迁移到领域适应的SLM中，挑战了这些策略在可信度保持方面的有效性假设，为高风险场景下SLM领域适应策略的选择提供了重要实证依据。

Abstract: Domain adaptation of small language models (SLMs) has emerged as a practical strategy for deploying capable NLP systems in resource-constrained, high-stakes environments including healthcare, legal services, and financial analysis. While performance gains from parameter-efficient fine-tuning are well characterised, the corresponding impact on trustworthiness (factual calibration and adversarial robustness) remains poorly understood. This paper presents the first systematic cross-domain, cross-architecture empirical study quantifying the trustworthiness cost of domain adaptation across three SLM architectures (TinyLlama 1B, Gemma-2 2B, Llama 3.2 1B), three domains (healthcare, legal, finance), two training-data conditions (benign and adversarially perturbed), and four fine-tuning strategies (baseline LoRA, Safety-DPO, Dark Experience Replay, and Task Arithmetic LoRA, TA-LoRA). Trustworthiness is evaluated through TruthfulQA MC2 (factual calibration) and HarmBench ASR (adversarial robustness) across all 216 experimental configurations with three random seeds. Three principal findings emerge. First, baseline QLoRA domain adaptation produces minimal TruthfulQA MC2 change across all model-domain combinations (mean |Delta TQA| < 0.02). Second, adversarially perturbed training data consistently improves domain adaptation quality (Delta loss approximately -0.040) without worsening trustworthiness benchmarks. Third, none of the three safety-preserving strategies reduced adversarial harm susceptibility: Safety-DPO was effectively neutral (mean Delta ASR < 0.001), while Dark ER and TA-LoRA increased mean HarmBench ASR by +0.171 and +0.155 respectively in safety-aligned models (Gemma-2 2B, Llama 3.2 1B), with individual configurations exceeding +0.45. These results challenge the assumption that replay-based and arithmetic-merge strategies transfer alignment to domain-adapted SLMs.

</details>


### [293] [Predicting Startup Exit from Textual Descriptors - A Computational Linguistics Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00045)
*Alberto M. G. Saruggia,Sebastien Germano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究证明仅凭文本描述即可预测初创企业退出（Exit）成功，无需依赖背景、财务或人力资本变量。基于20年7,419家初创企业的风投数据集，构建850个文本特征，LightGBM达到最高F1=0.48，纯文本特征F1=0.30。研究发现适度"炒作"标记（形容词、行话、流行词）与更高的退出概率相关，而过长的陈述或名称则降低概率，并引入了可量化的"炒作评分"（Hyping Score）工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高信息不对称条件下，早期初创企业的投资决策极为困难。此前研究通常依赖财务和人力资本等结构化数据，而创始人叙事文本这一丰富的信息来源未被充分挖掘。本文旨在验证纯文本描述能否独立预测初创企业成功，从而为风险投资决策提供新的信号来源。

Method: 使用风投策展的数据集（涵盖20年、7,419家初创企业），通过初创企业叙事映射工程化850个特征。先后进行数据子集和向量嵌入的统计显著性评估，再使用六种监督机器学习模型进行实验对比，最终通过特征分析确定预测性文本标记及其最适密度。

Result: LightGBM在全部特征下取得最高预测性能（F1=0.48），仅使用文本描述符时F1=0.30，验证了创始人叙事的独立预测价值。特征分析显示，"炒作"标记（形容词、行话、流行词）的最适密度与更高的退出概率相关，而过长陈述或名称长度则产生负面影响。此外，提出了可应用的风险投资"炒作评分"指标。

Conclusion: 初创企业的文本框架提供了在高信息不对称条件下预测退出成功的可测信号。叙事语言中适度的"炒作"元素具有预测价值，而该研究开创性地证明了纯文本描述可独立用于早期投资决策辅助，为风险投资领域引入了新的量化分析维度。

Abstract: This study shows that textual descriptors alone can predict early-stage startup success, defined as Exit, without relying on contextual, financial, or human capital variables. Using venture capital-curated datasets covering 7,419 startups over 20 years, the research isolates text-based framing variables and engineers 850 features through startup narrative mapping. Data subsets and vector embeddings are evaluated for statistical significance, followed by supervised machine learning experiments across six models. LightGBM achieved the highest predictive performance (F1 = 0.48), while textual descriptors alone achieved F1 = 0.30, confirming the standalone predictive value of founder narratives. Feature analysis shows that optimized densities of hyping markers, including adjectives, jargon, and buzzwords, are associated with higher Exit probability, whereas excessive statement or name length reduces it. The study also introduces a quantifiable Hyping Score for venture capital applications, demonstrating that startup framing provides measurable signals for predicting Exit under conditions of high information asymmetry.

</details>


### [294] [Neural Circuit Function Inference with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00059)
*Yijie Yin,Albert Cardona*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LLantia的自动化方法，利用大语言模型结合连接组数据，从文献中提取细胞类型功能描述，推断神经回路功能及组成细胞类型的作用，并以果蝇大脑为案例进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接组图谱的成功将理解神经系统的挑战转移到了神经回路的解读上，需要系统化、自动化的方法从连接组数据中推断神经回路功能及其细胞类型角色。

Method: LLantia方法首先从文献中提炼细胞类型功能的描述，结合连接组信息，推断所有其他细胞类型的功能，以此为基础进一步推断神经回路功能。结果以层级结构组织，不同可能的回路功能按行为和生理学情境分类，每个回路功能包含子回路描述和相关细胞类型。

Result: 该方法成功推断出成年果蝇大脑所有细胞类型的功能及若干更广泛的回路，并通过与分析方法发布之后新发表的文献交叉验证，验证了结果的准确性。

Conclusion: LLantia提供了一种可扩展的自动化神经回路解读方案，支持回溯至已发表的已知信息，并为后续实验研究提供了基础。

Abstract: The success of connectome mapping now shifts the challenge of understanding the nervous system to the interpretation of neural circuits. Here, we devise a new automated method, LLantia (LLM automated neural circuit inference and analysis), to systematically infer neural circuit function and the role of its component neural cell types. Our approach distills descriptions of cell type function from the literature and, in combination with the connectome, then infers the function for all other cell types, which serves as a basis for subsequent neural circuit function inference. Results are structured hierarchically, with different possible circuit functions organised under multiple possible behavioural and physiological contexts, and each circuit function composed of subcircuit descriptions alongside relevant cell types to facilitate both backtracking to known, published information and support further experimental research. We illustrate our method by inferring cell type function for all cell types of the adult fruit fly brain and for select broader circuits within, and validate our findings, including by cross-checking with literature published after the release date of our analysis.

</details>


### [295] [LLM-OSDA: An Optimal-Stopping Dynamic Auction for Native Advertising in Multi-Turn LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00123)
*Yan Fang,Jialin Chen,Chun Gan,Hang Yu,Mingjun Nie,Yeyu Zhang,Fengxiang He,Ching Law*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-OSDA，一个将最优停止理论、赢家分配和包络定价整合到动态点击付费拍卖中的机制，用于LLM原生广告。该机制通过贝叶斯最优停止决策决定广告插入时机，并证明其分配单调性与真实报价弱占优性。实验显示相比固定时机基线提升11%净收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM广告拍卖机制仅在单次响应内运作，决定赢家但不决定广告插入的时机。由于每次会话只有一次原生广告插入机会，停止时间依赖于竞价，将时序与分配耦合，使得静态真实性论证不再适用。因此需要新的动态拍卖机制来解决时机决策问题。

Method: 提出LLM-OSDA框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于Bellman最优停止的动态决策，决定何时插入广告；(2) 赢家分配机制；(3) 包络定价。一个与出价无关的LLM层估计上下文点击质量并渲染广告，而出价仅进入拍卖机制。实际部署中用StopNet网络近似Bellman动作价值函数。

Result: 在精确Bellman预言机下，期望折扣点击分配对每个广告主的出价是单调的，对应包络支付使真实出价在期望意义上是弱占优策略。近似分析表明，StopNet的决策仅在停止边界附近偏离最优策略，激励损失可由近似误差界定。模拟会话广告语料实验显示，LLM-OSDA比最强固定时机基线净收入提升11%，同时保持相当的用户留存。

Conclusion: LLM-OSDA成功将动态最优停止理论引入LLM原生广告拍卖，解决了时机与分配耦合下的激励兼容问题，并在实践中通过近似方法实现可观收益提升，为该领域提供了新的拍卖范式和理论保障。

Abstract: LLM-native advertising embeds sponsored content directly into model-generated responses, shifting the unit of sale from a fixed slot to a moment within an evolving conversation. Existing LLM ad-auction mechanisms primarily operate within a single response, settling the winner but not the timing. The extension is nontrivial: with one native insertion opportunity per session, the stopping time depends on bids, coupling timing with allocation, so static truthfulness arguments no longer apply. We propose the LLM-based Optimal Stopping Dynamic Auction (LLM-OSDA), a dynamic cost-per-click auction that integrates Bellman optimal stopping, winner allocation, and envelope pricing. A bid-independent LLM layer estimates contextual click quality and seamlessly renders the winning ad, while bids enter only the committed auction mechanism. Under an exact Bellman oracle, the expected discounted-click allocation is monotone in each advertiser's bid, and the corresponding envelope payment makes truthful bidding weakly dominant in expectation. For practical deployment, a learned StopNet approximates the Bellman action values. We show that its decisions differ from the optimal policy only near the stopping boundary and bound the resulting incentive loss in terms of its approximation error. Experiments on a simulated conversational advertising corpus show that LLM-OSDA improves net revenue by 11 percent over the strongest fixed-timing baseline while maintaining comparable user retention. Code is at https://github.com/2025Fang2025/llm-osda.

</details>


### [296] [DiffusionGemma Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00146)
*DiffusionGemma Team,Adrien Ali Taïga,James Assiene,Daniele Calandriello,Rahma Chaabouni,João Gante,Tamara von Glehn,Nate Keating,Chris Knutsen,Martin Kukla,Tianlin Liu,Ivan Lobov,Ofir Nabati,João Gabriel Oliveira,Nicolas Perez-Nieves,Nastasia Prutianova,Bobak Shahriari,Jean Tarbouriech,Pavel Tyletski,Çağlar Ünlü,Cindy Wu,Glenn Cameron,Jerome Connor,Sertan Girgin,Maarten Grootendorst,Alon Levkovitch,Eliya Nachmani,Omar Sanseviero,Piotr Stanczyk,Quentin Berthet,Andrew Campbell,Clément Crepy,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet,Romuald Elie,Alexandre Galashov,Klaus Greff,Alexis Jacq,David Ruhe,Yu-Han Wu,Sebastian Flennerhag,Brendan O'Donoghue,George Scrivener,Shantanu Thakoor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DiffusionGemma，一个基于离散扩散的开源语言模型，通过并行迭代细化256个token块来生成文本，而非逐token自回归解码。该模型通过对Gemma 4（MoE，3.8B激活/25.2B总参数）进行高效微调获得，训练预算不足原始AR模型token的10%。采用两阶段训练管线：监督微调教授双向去噪，联合强化学习与采样器蒸馏提升生成质量和推理效率。在单张H100上可达约1500 tokens/秒，建立了生成速度与模型能力之间的新Pareto前沿，同时保留思考模式、多模态和长上下文支持，并能以轻微性能损失进行AR生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归（AR）大语言模型受限于逐token顺序解码，形成推理速度瓶颈。作者希望通过离散扩散模型并行生成文本，以突破这一瓶颈，实现显著更快的文本生成速度。

Method: 采用计算高效的两阶段训练管线：(1) 监督微调阶段教授双向去噪能力；(2) 第二阶段结合强化学习与采样器蒸馏，联合优化生成质量和推理效率。模型基于Gemma 4 MoE模型微调获得，并行细化256个token块。

Result: DiffusionGemma在完整评估套件上平均每次前向传播生成约20个token，在单张NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现约1500 tokens/秒的输出速度，显著快于即使采用最先进推测解码的AR模型，建立了生成速度与模型能力之间的新Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 扩散微调后的模型仍保持AR生成能力（仅轻微性能退化），揭示了混合扩散-AR解码的可能路径；同时保留了思考模式、多模态输入和长上下文支持，展示了离散扩散作为高效语言生成范式的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce DiffusionGemma, an experimental open-weight language model that uses discrete diffusion to generate text at exceptionally high speed. Rather than decoding one token at a time, DiffusionGemma iteratively refines blocks of 256 tokens in parallel, avoiding the sequential decoding bottleneck of conventional autoregressive (AR) large language models. Instead of training from scratch, we obtain DiffusionGemma by fine-tuning the mixture-of-experts Gemma 4 model with 3.8B activated and 25.2B total parameters. Our compute-efficient two-stage training pipeline uses fewer than 10% of the starting AR model's total training token budget. The first stage uses supervised fine-tuning to teach bidirectional denoising, while the second stage combines reinforcement learning with sampler distillation to jointly improve generation quality and inference efficiency. DiffusionGemma establishes a new Pareto frontier for the trade-off between generation speed and model capability. Averaged across our full evaluation suite, it generates around 20 tokens per forward pass and achieves roughly 1,500 output tokens per second on a single NVIDIA H100 GPU, which is substantially faster than AR models even with state-of-the-art speculative decoding. DiffusionGemma also retains the starting model's support for thinking mode, multimodal inputs, and long contexts. Despite diffusion fine-tuning, it remains capable of AR generation with only minor performance degradation, suggesting a path toward hybrid diffusion-AR decoding.

</details>


### [297] [A Constitution-Grid Instrument for Data-Efficient RL Alignment (C-Guard)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00180)
*Lily Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出C-Guard和C-LIM方法，用于解决RL对齐中安全守卫训练的矛盾目标（捕捉真实危害与不拒绝良性提示）的数据效率问题。通过宪法网格生成训练数据，并用可学习性评分决定每格操作，将过度拒绝率从22.4%降至12.8%，同时提升了数据区域的学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL对齐中训练安全守卫需要优化两个相互冲突的目标：捕捉真实危害和不拒绝良性提示。在矛盾目标上进行数据高效训练是困难的，现有方法在过度拒绝与拒绝不足之间存在权衡困境。

Method: 提出C-Guard，一种宪法网格（constitution-grid）工具用于生成RL训练数据；提出C-LIM，一种逐单元格可学习性评分（per-cell learnability score），决定每个单元格的操作为修剪（prune）、加密（densify）、修正（amend）或扩展（expand）。在投入训练预算前，C-LIM即可标记无效数据区域。

Result: 过度拒绝率从22.4%改善至12.8%，而对抗性攻击下的拒绝不足从0.27轻微恶化至0.33。187行无目标数据未带来任何收益，该方法将同一区域的学习影响从0.733提升至0.80。

Conclusion: C-Guard与C-LIM框架通过提前识别并处理无效数据区域，有效提升了RL安全训练的数据效率，相关代码和宪法已开源。

Abstract: Conflicting objectives are general in RL alignment, and training on them data-efficiently is hard. Training a safety guard with RL means optimizing two objectives that conflict: catch real harm, and do not refuse benign prompts. Our finding is that over-refusal improves 22.4% to 12.8%, while under-refusal on adversarial attacks silently worsens 0.27 to 0.33. We present C-Guard, a constitution-grid instrument that generates the RL training data, and C-LIM, a per-cell learnability score that decides each cell's move: prune, densify, amend, expand. C-LIM flags the dead-weight data region before any training budget is spent: 187 untargeted rows had bought zero gain, and our method lifts the same region's learning impact 0.733 to 0.80. Code and the constitution are open-sourced.

</details>


### [298] [Averaging Bias: Human Faithfulness Annotations are not Locally Faithful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00205)
*Huajian Zhang,Yiyang Feng,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现广泛使用的文本摘要忠实度基准中的人类标注存在"平均偏差"（Averaging Bias），即人类并非严格按照"所有句子都必须有据可依"的合取规则来标注，而是倾向于在大多数句子忠实时就判定摘要为忠实。通过五个大语言模型裁判对四个基准的逐句评估，研究发现全局人类标签与逐句LLM判断的平均值相关性更高，且人工复查确认大量被标为忠实的摘要中存在真实的局部事实错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本摘要忠实度评估的严格合取规则要求摘要中每一个句子都被源文档支持才算忠实，但现有基准通常只收集一个全局人类标注标签。作者质疑这种全局人类标签是否真正实现了合取规则，并假设人类可能倾向于在"大多数句子忠实"而非"所有句子忠实"时接受摘要为忠实。

Method: 使用五个大语言模型（LLM）作为逐句评分器（per-sentence raters），在四个广泛使用的忠实度基准上对摘要进行逐句评估；将全局人类标签分别与"逐句LLM判断的平均值"和"严格合取规则实施结果"进行相关性比较；并通过人工复查验证被人类标记为忠实的摘要中是否存在局部事实错误。

Result: 研究发现全局人类标签与逐句LLM判断的平均值相关性优于与严格合取规则实施结果的相关性；人工复查证实相当比例被人类标注为忠实的摘要中确实包含真实的局部事实错误，证实了Averaging Bias的存在。

Conclusion: 广泛使用的忠实度基准中的人类标注存在可测量的平均偏差，这种偏差表明当前基准的人类标注并未严格实现合取规则，因此需要精心设计的标注结构来获取可信赖的人类标注。

Abstract: Evaluation of faithfulness of text summarization treats a model generated summary as faithful only if every of its sentences is supported by the source document: a strict conjunctive rule under which a single unsupported sentence makes the whole summary unfaithful. Yet most faithfulness benchmarks collect only one global human annotation label per summary. We ask whether such global human labels actually implement the conjunctive rule. We hypothesize that annotators may accept a summary as faithful when most sentences are faithful, not only when all are faithful. To test our hypothesis, we use five large language model (LLM) judges as per-sentence raters across four widely used faithfulness benchmarks. We find that global human labels correlate better with the average of per-sentence LLM judgments than with the implementation of the strict conjunctive rule. A manual review confirms that a substantial fraction of summaries labeled faithful by humans contain genuine local factual errors. We call this tendency Averaging Bias. Our results reveal that human labels on widely used faithfulness benchmarks contain measurable Averaging Bias, calling for carefully structured designs for trustworthy human annotations

</details>


### [299] [Bridging the English-Arabic Medical Knowledge Gap: Targeted Low-Rank Adaptation via Causal Layer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00207)
*Chaimae Abouzahir,Musa Khan,Hala Ali-Hassan,Congbo Ma,Khaled Saleh,Yousra Sadqi,Jihad Mallat,Walid Al-Eisawi,Nizar Habash,Farah E. Shamout*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过机制分析发现阿拉伯语医学知识存在于LLM中间层表征中但无法在输出层呈现，据此提出仅针对表征发散层窗口进行低秩适配（TLoRA）的目标适应策略，在阿拉伯语医学问答、生成和临床对话任务上表现优异，并引入新的阿拉伯语临床对话基准AraClinicDialog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在英语医学任务上表现良好，但在阿拉伯语上性能显著下降，此前被广泛归因于训练数据不足。作者希望系统性地验证这一假设的准确性，并探索更具针对性的适应策略。

Method: 使用tuned lens探针和因果激活修补（causal activation patching）进行机制分析，发现阿拉伯语医学知识存在于中间层但未在输出层显现。据此提出TLoRA（Targeted Low-Rank Adaptation），仅在跨语言表征发散的层窗口中进行低秩适配，而非全网络微调。同时构建了ArabClinicDialog基准，包含标准阿拉伯语及四种方言变体。

Result: 在多选题医学问答任务上，TLoRA优于全网络LoRA、零样本和少样本基线；在短答案生成和多轮临床对话任务上表现具竞争力且无需任务特定微调。机制诊断方法可作为资源匮乏语言医学LLM目标适应的实用指南。

Conclusion: 证明了机制诊断（mechanistic diagnosis）可以实际指导低资源语言医学LLM的定向适配，挑战了"数据不足"这一传统归因，提供了更高效、更精准的模型适应方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly in English medical tasks but degrade substantially in Arabic, a gap widely attributed to limited training data. We systematically investigate this assumption via tuned lens probing and causal activation patching, and find that Arabic medical knowledge is present in intermediate model representations but fails to surface at the output. This mechanistic insight motivates a targeted adaptation strategy: rather than fine-tuning the full network, we propose Targeted Low-Rank Adaptation (TLoRA), restricted to the layer window where cross-lingual representations diverge, upstream of the output layers where the failure manifests. We evaluate TLoRA on multiple-choice medical QA, where our approach outperforms full-network LoRA, zero-shot, and few-shot baselines. We further evaluate it on short-answer generation and multi-turn clinical dialogue, where it performs competitively without the need for task-specific finetuning. We additionally introduce AraClinicDialog, a clinician-constructed Arabic medical dialogue benchmark in MSA with validated variants across four Arabic dialects. Together, these contributions demonstrate that mechanistic diagnosis can serve as a practical guide for targeted adaptation in underrepresented-language medical LLMs.

</details>


### [300] [A Few Neurons Reveal When LLMs Misuse Tools: Sparse Detection and Selective Steering for Reliable Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00218)
*Yutong Ke,Ming Yin,Chongwen Zhao,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISMS框架，利用特定于工具调用失败的MLP神经元进行稀疏检测和激活引导，可检测LLM工具调用的三类失败（无效参数、过度调用、遗漏调用），并通过条件干预将过度调用率降低80%，工具需求准确率提升14.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM在工具调用中存在三类关键失败模式——无效参数、不必要的过度调用、以及需要工具时遗漏调用。作者发现这些失败与特定的MLP神经元子集相关，且可线性分离，这为轻量级检测和干预提供了可能性。

Method: PRISMS是一个闭环框架，包含两部分：(1) 选择对失败贡献关键的MLP神经元，在这些神经元的激活上拟合L1正则化检测器；(2) 共享同一神经元基础进行激活引导。检测在提示边界或生成的工具调用片段上进行，并根据预测失败风险门控干预以避免无条件引导的副作用。

Result: 在Qwen3、Llama和Gemma三个模型家族的六个模型上：过度调用和遗漏调用在提示边界以ROC-AUC 0.90-1.00被检测，无效参数在工具调用片段以ROC-AUC 0.86-0.90被检测。仅需1-128个神经元，比密集残差流基线少23-627倍特征。PRISMS将池化过度调用率从0.131降至0.026（降低80%），工具需求准确率从0.689提升至0.831（+14.2个百分点）。

Conclusion: PRISMS证明了跨模型家族的工具调用失败可以通过极小规模的MLP神经元子集进行高效、稀疏的检测，并且共享的神经元基础支持双向行为控制。通过风险门控干预，在减小副作用的同时显著改善了工具调用的整体可靠性，为轻量级LLM监控与引导提供了可推广的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic LLMs exhibit three consequential tool-use failures: invalid arguments (validity), unnecessary calls (over-calling), and omitted calls when tools are needed (missing). We find that a small, failure-specific set of MLP neurons could distinguish such failures with linearly separable decision boundaries. Building on this observation, we introduce PRISMS (Probing Representations In Support of Monitoring and Steering), a closed-loop framework that shares a failure-specific neuron basis between sparse detection and activation steering. PRISMS selects contribution-critical MLP neurons and fits an L1-regularized detector on their activations. Across six models from the Qwen3, Llama, and Gemma families, over-calling and missing are detected at the pre-generation prompt boundary with ROC-AUC 0.90-1.00, while validity is detected from the generated tool-call span with ROC-AUC 0.86-0.90. These results are achieved with highly sparse readouts: only 1-2 MLP neurons for missing, 2-16 for over-calling, and approximately 128 for validity. These sparse detectors match or outperform dense residual-stream baselines using 23-627 times fewer features. The shared neuron basis also supports bidirectional control over tool-calling behavior, suppressing unnecessary calls and eliciting omitted ones. PRISMS therefore gates intervention on predicted failure risk to mitigate the collateral effects of unconditional steering. Across all six models, PRISMS reduces pooled over-calling rate by 80% (from 0.131 to 0.026) while increasing tool-required accuracy by 14.2 percentage points (from 0.689 to 0.831). PRISMS thus provides lightweight failure detection and selective intervention across model families.

</details>


### [301] [Cross-Task Dissociation in Frontier Vision-Language Model Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00261)
*Kejia Zhang,Youran Sun,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了九个前沿视觉语言模型在两个心理学基准任务上的心理理论（Theory-of-Mind）表现，发现模型间不存在一致的心智理论档案，不同任务上表现差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究前沿视觉语言模型是否在心理理论任务上表现出与人类参考群体一致且连贯的表现，还是因范式不同而呈现碎片化特征。

Method: 使用9个前沿VLM，在Keysar导演任务（自我中心干扰下的视觉观点采择）和Frith-Happé动画三角任务（纯运动中的意图归因）上进行评估，并与人类参考组（成人、儿童、典型发展成人、高功能自闭症成人）进行比较。

Result: 导演任务中，无思维链时模型在78%试次中犯自我中心错误，类似儿童而非成人；三角任务中，模型的意图归因低于预期，其心理理论档案与高功能自闭症成人均值更接近（超过三倍），而目标导向和随机运动接近典型发展成人。没有任何模型在两个任务上都最接近典型发展成人。

Conclusion: VLM的心理理论能力在不同任务范式间缺乏一致性，呈现碎片化特征；报告仅提供群体层面的描述，不构成任何模型的诊断标签。

Abstract: Do frontier vision-language models present a coherent Theory-of-Mind (ToM) profile across tasks, matching the same human reference group, or does that profile fragment from one paradigm to the next? We evaluate a shared panel of nine frontier VLMs on two psychology-derived benchmarks: the Keysar Director Task (visual perspective-taking under egocentric interference) and the Frith-Happé animated triangles scored with the Castelli rubric (intention attribution from pure motion). On the Director Task, without chain-of-thought, the panel makes the egocentric error on 78\% of trials like children rather than adults; variation is substantial across models, and reasoning rescues several models. On the triangles, the panel under-attributes intention: its ToM profile sits more than three times closer to the high-functioning-autistic-adult (HF-ASD) mean than to the typical-development-adult (TD) mean, while Goal-Directed and Random stay near TD. No model is nearest TD on both tasks; the model that looks adult-like on the Director Task falls on the HF-ASD side on the triangles, and the most TD-like model on the triangles is child-like on the Director Task. We report group-level descriptions, not diagnostic labels for any model.

</details>


### [302] [Sixteen models, fewer than two voices: measuring ensemble dispersion where no answer is uniquely correct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00285)
*Mario Vega-Barbas,Lidia Mora-Valenciano,Iván Pau,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过16个语言模型（来自10个家族）对心理治疗案例进行表述，测量集成模型输出的语义多样性。使用Vendi Score量化多样性，并提出了每个模型对集成分歧贡献的新指标。通过预注册的假设检验发现，模型身份确实影响分歧，但传统分类（规模、家族）只能部分解释结构；分歧与临床内容有关，而非预期的解释开放性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，多样性和不确定性指标都通过正确性标准来验证，但在模型集成的情境中，正确性标准并不适用（该任务无法定义唯一的正确答案）。此外，现有聚合指标无法揭示多样性的来源——即哪些模型贡献了分歧。研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用来自10个模型家族的16个语言模型，对15个分层设计的小型案例（vignettes）进行表述，共产生7,082条表述。使用Vendi Score（相似度矩阵的冯·诺依曼熵指数）衡量多样性。提出一个新的"per-model dissent contribution"指标——模型对集成中其他成员的平均相似度的补数。通过预注册假设检验，测试模型身份是否对分歧方差贡献非零份额。

Result: 模型平均产生1.69个不同表述（单个模型的基线为1.43）。模型身份是可检测的分歧结构化因素，但传统类别只能部分解释：规模差异在不同模型中方向相反，家族仅在5条双成员线上分组，最分歧的声音随集成组成而变化。分歧与临床内容相关，而非与案例库分层的解释开放性相关。

Conclusion: 集成分散性应作为需要测量的属性而非假设的属性。传统的模型分类（规模、家族）不足以预测哪些模型会产生分歧，分歧的组织结构由临床内容驱动而非解释开放性。涌现的离群者描述的是整体的组成特征，而非模型本身。

Abstract: Sixteen language models drawn from ten families produced, on average, the semantic diversity of 1.69 distinct formulations of a psychotherapeutic case, against a single-model baseline of 1.43 from one model's own runs. Ensembles place more than one reading before a decision-maker on the premise that several models supply several perspectives. Dispersion over their outputs is measured both as diversity and as uncertainty, and both traditions validate it against a correctness criterion that this task does not admit. Measuring diversity is a solved problem: the Vendi Score, the exponential of the von Neumann entropy of a similarity matrix, is an effective number of distinct elements. What a single aggregate does not say is where the diversity comes from. We define a per-model dissent contribution, the complement of a model's mean similarity to the other members of its ensemble: a magnitude from the same matrix, not a decomposition of the spectral index, whose maximum identifies the most divergent voice. Crossing model and case, we test as a preregistered hypothesis whether model identity accounts for a non-zero share of the variance in dissent, and characterise the structure that test detects. The panel formulated fifteen stratified vignettes, yielding 7,082 formulations for analysis. Model identity was a detectable structuring factor of the dissent that remained, but the usual categories recovered it only partly: scale differences pointed in opposite directions across pairs, family grouped models on only five two-member lines, and the most divergent voice changed with panel composition, so that the surfaced outlier describes the ensemble rather than the model. Dissent did not track the interpretive openness for which the case bank was stratified; it was organised by clinical content instead, leaving the dispersion an ensemble produces a property to measure rather than assume.

</details>


### [303] [Comparing and Modeling Argumentation in German Political Communication across Arenas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00288)
*Nina Vikhrova,Johannes Kühling,Sebastian Haunss,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对政治论证在不同政治舞台（全体会议演讲、委员会会议、新闻发布会）中的模式差异问题，构建了一个17k句子的德语语料库（主题固定为COVID-19），标注了论证段落及其边界和类别，并进行了自动识别论证段落的试点研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治论证在民主决策中至关重要，但不同政治舞台（如全体会议和委员会会议）的论证模式存在显著差异。然而，尽管论证研究受到广泛关注，计算层面分析不同政治舞台间论证模式差异的工作仍然很少，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建了包含17,000个句子的语料库，覆盖德语三种政治舞台（全体会议演讲、委员会会议、新闻发布会），保持主题（COVID-19）恒定。语料标注了论证段落的边界和类别（论证及其理由）。随后进行了自动识别论证段落的试点研究。

Result: 语料分析发现与预期相反——领域专业知识的论证在新闻发布会中比委员会会议中更频繁。自动识别试点研究结果表明，论证边界难以精确定位，模型预测还存在确认偏差问题。

Conclusion: 本文填补了政治论证计算分析的研究空白，提供了新的跨政治舞台论证语料库，揭示了对论证模式的直觉认知与实证结果之间的差异，并指出了自动论证识别面临的挑战（边界模糊和确认偏差）。

Abstract: Deliberation, involving the formulation and exchange of arguments, forms an integral part of political decision making in democracies. Argumentation patterns however differ substantially across different political arenas, such as plenary speeches and committee meetings. However, despite a lot of interest in argumentation, there is comparatively little computational work on analyzing differences in patterns of political argumentation between arenas.
  Our work addresses this research gap. First, we present a 17k-sentence corpus with annotation for argumentative passages (argument and their justifications, both their boundaries and their categories) across three German political arenas (plenary speeches, committee meetings, and press conferences), keeping the topic (COVID-19) constant. Our analysis of the corpus finds that contrary to expectations, justification by domain-specific expertise is more frequent in press conferences than in committee meetings. Second, we present a pilot study on automatically identifying such argumentative passages. The results show that boundaries are hard to pin down, and models predictions additionally suffer from confirmation bias.

</details>


### [304] [SeDeM: Selective Decompression of Hidden-State Memories for Long-Context Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00311)
*Maryam Haghifam,Jason Cong,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SeDeM是一个选择性解压缩框架，将紧凑记忆存储与解码器条件控制解耦，通过从中间Transformer层提取隐藏状态、轻量压缩器存储为记忆块、查询条件选择器挑选相关块、解压器仅扩展选中块，从而避免全上下文处理和直接从高度压缩的记忆槽生成。在四个长上下文QA基准上，SeDeM在1B和3B同骨干设置下均优于压缩基线，3B骨干在三个数据集上超过全上下文微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理成本高昂：自注意力在预填充阶段随序列长度二次方增长，KV缓存随处理token数增长，且更大上下文窗口并不保证可靠的证据使用。许多软压缩方法依赖LLM作为压缩器并依赖紧凑记忆token来同时保留信息和条件化解码器，存在局限性。

Method: SeDeM框架包含四个核心组件：1) LLM从选定的中间Transformer层提取隐藏状态；2) 轻量压缩器将这些状态存储为记忆块；3) 查询条件选择器选择相关记忆块（训练中使用块级证据监督）；4) 解压器仅将选中的块扩展为与中间解码器层兼容的隐藏状态。这样就实现了记忆存储与解码器条件控制的解耦。

Result: 在四个长上下文QA基准上，SeDeM在1B和3B同骨干设置下均获得比评估的压缩基线更高的QA分数；3B骨干在三个数据集上超越全上下文微调；减少了在线首token延迟时间，并相对于ICAE提高了自回归解码吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过解耦记忆存储与解码器条件控制，SeDeM有效避免了全上下文处理和直接从高度压缩记忆槽生成的弊端，在长上下文QA任务上取得优于压缩基线的效果，同时改善了推理效率。

Abstract: Long-context inference with large language models (LLMs) is costly: self-attention during prefill scales quadratically with sequence length, and the key-value (KV) cache grows with the number of processed tokens. Larger context windows also do not ensure reliable evidence use. Context compression reduces this cost, but many soft-compression methods use LLMs as compressors and rely on compact memory tokens both to preserve information and to condition the decoder. We propose SeDeM, a selective decompression framework that decouples compact memory storage from decoder conditioning. An LLM extracts hidden states from a chosen intermediate Transformer layer, a lightweight compressor stores them as memory blocks, a query-conditioned selector selects relevant blocks, and a decompressor expands only the selected blocks into hidden states compatible with an intermediate decoder layer. Thus, the decoder avoids both full-context processing and direct generation from highly compressed memory slots. On four long-context QA benchmarks, SeDeM achieves higher QA scores than the evaluated compression baselines in both 1B and 3B same-backbone settings, and with the 3B backbone exceeds full-context fine-tuning on three datasets. The learned selector uses block-level evidence supervision during training. SeDeM also reduces online time-to-first-token and improves autoregressive decoding throughput relative to ICAE.

</details>


### [305] [CurveShift: Is Agent Progress Scalar? Separating Level from Shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00355)
*Hanwen Xing,Pengyun Wang,BingXu Meng,Kumail Alhamoud,Xiang Li,Jicheng Wang,Xin Yu,Xinyang Han,Xiaomin Li,Philip Torr,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型性能向难题转移的表象大部分可由能力提升和天花板效应解释，而非能力结构发生质变。通过LiveCodeBench基准（无智能体脚手架、时间配对模型与外部难度排序）打破混杂因素后，确认存在一个较小但真实的难题增益效应：2024年9月后发布的模型在最高难度问题上额外获得约+0.40 logits的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM进展总结常用单一标量度量（如时间跨度、潜在能力估计或聚合基准分数），这些无法检验进步是否在不同任务难度间分布不均。作者希望验证"进步向更难任务集中"这一现象是否真实存在，而非度量方法带来的伪影，同时解决智能体基准中模型与脚手架混淆的问题。

Method: 使用Rasch模型分离整体能力提升与难度-响应曲线形状变化；首先在METR时间跨度数据上检验天花板效应是否解释表面上的难题增益。然后在LiveCodeBench（公共竞技编程基准，无智能体脚手架，66个时间配对模型×1,055道题）上，以外部难度排序为基准，在控制整体能力上升后检验2024年9月后模型在最高难度题目上是否有超出预测的额外增益。

Result: METR数据上，单一Rasch模型（仅能力上升）即可复现大部分表现上的难题增益，属天花板效应而非能力质变。但在LiveCodeBench上，控制整体能力后，2024年9月后发布的模型在最难题目上仍额外获得约+0.40 logits增益（最保守假设下），使难题求解率从约18%提升到25%。该效应主要由最强推理模型驱动，且对需要短时推理而非长程自治的难题成立。

Conclusion: 难题增益效应存在但比表象小得多——大部分看似向难题的进步的转移其实由整体能力提升和天花板效应造成。真实的难题增益在竞争编程领域可干净识别，由最强推理模型驱动，但不应泛化为所有任务类型。作者发布LiveCodeBench难度面板（66个模型×1,055道题）及分析代码以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Progress in large language models is often summarized using a single scalar measure, such as a time horizon, a latent ability estimate, or an aggregate benchmark score. These summaries capture the overall performance, but they do not test whether progress is distributed differently across task difficulty. We find that most of the apparent shift in gains toward harder tasks does not reflect a change in the shape of the difficulty-response curve. On METR time-horizon data, a single Rasch model with rising ability reproduces this pattern, so it is largely explained by ceiling effects rather than a qualitative change in capability. This echoes how the choice of metric can make claimed emergent abilities look like a property of the models themselves. We then identify a smaller hard-task effect that survives this control. Isolating it is difficult on agentic benchmarks, because newer models are usually run with newer agentic harnesses, so a gain on hard tasks cannot be assigned to the model or its scaffold. We break the confound with LiveCodeBench, a public competitive programming benchmark that runs no agentic scaffold while pairing dated models with an exogenous difficulty ordering. After accounting for the rise in overall ability, models released after September 2024 still gain on the hardest problems beyond what their easy and medium performance predicts, by about +0.40 logits under our most conservative assumption, raising the hard-problem solve rate from roughly 18% to 25%. The effect is led by the strongest reasoning models and holds for hard tasks that need only short reasoning, not autonomy over long horizons. We present this as a result specific to competitive programming, since our clean identification rests on a single coding benchmark. We release the LiveCodeBench Difficulty Panel (66 dated models x 1,055 problems) and our analysis code.

</details>


### [306] [Deep Research Pretraining via Predictive Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00432)
*Jiang Zhou,Zhiyuan Fan,Xing Wu,Tinghao Yu,Feng Zhang,Lilin Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Deep Research Pretraining (DRP)框架，利用自然出现的引用/超链接结构构建代理研究目标，生成搜索-打开-写作轨迹进行离线预训练，无需昂贵的环境交互数据。在Qwen3-14B-Base上验证，DRP在低数据预算下显著优于基线模型，且效果可迁移至多个基准任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体通常依赖于昂贵的、需要真实环境交互的工具使用轨迹数据进行训练，这些数据需要反复检索、文档检查和报告评估，获取成本极高。作者希望找到一种无需实时检索环境和执行策略即可训练研究智能体的替代方法。

Method: DRP从带有引用或超链接的文本中构建代理研究目标，恢复链接证据和图相关替代方案，并将其转化为"搜索-打开-写作"轨迹。在学术引用图(DRP-Paper)和维基百科超链接(DRP-Web)上实例化，用1B tokens持续预训练Qwen3-14B-Base，再在13K智能体轨迹的受控比例上进行微调。

Result: 在五个独立低数据采样子集上，DRP两个变体均一致优于匹配的无DRP基线模型。仅用四分之一的SFT数据，DRP-Web即超过完整数据训练的无DRP检查点，且在ResearchQA、WebWalkerQA和SimpleQA上均有增益提升。DRP-Web的优势在后续智能体RL阶段也能保持。

Conclusion: 消融实验（源匹配和证据不匹配对照）表明改进来自证据条件化导航而非领域暴露或智能体格式模仿。DRP为基于轨迹的智能体训练提供了有前景的补充方法。

Abstract: Deep research agents are often trained on expensive, environment-grounded tool-use trajectories that require repeated retrieval, document inspection, and report evaluation. We introduce Deep Research Pretraining (DRP), an offline framework that derives predictive navigation supervision from naturally occurring evidence structures. Given a citation-bearing or hyperlinked passage, DRP constructs a proxy research objective, recovers linked evidence and graph-related alternatives, and converts them into search-open-write trajectories. This teaches models what to search for, which documents to inspect, and how to synthesize evidence, without a live retrieval environment or executed policy rollout.
